--- alembic/env.py ---
from logging.config import fileConfig
from sqlalchemy import pool
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_engine_from_config
from alembic import context
import sys
import asyncio

sys.path.insert(0, '/data/data/com.termux/files/home/waste-platform')

config = context.config

if config.config_file_name is not None:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base
from app.shared.infrastructure.db import register_models  # noqa: F401
target_metadata = Base.metadata

def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    url = config.get_main_option("sqlalchemy.url")
    context.configure(
        url=url,
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
    )
    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()

def do_run_migrations(connection):
    context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=target_metadata)
    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()

async def run_async_migrations():
    connectable = async_engine_from_config(
        config.get_section(config.config_ini_section, {}),
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )
    async with connectable.connect() as connection:
        await connection.run_sync(do_run_migrations)
    await connectable.dispose()

def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    asyncio.run(run_async_migrations())

if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()

from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base

--- app/shared/infrastructure/db/session.py ---
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncGenerator

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import (
    AsyncEngine,
    AsyncSession,
    async_sessionmaker,
    create_async_engine,
)

from app.core.config import settings


def _db_url() -> str:
    db = getattr(settings, "database", None)
    for attr in ("async_url", "url"):
        if db is not None:
            val = getattr(db, attr, None)
            if val:
                return str(val)
    return str(getattr(settings, "DATABASE_URL", "sqlite+aiosqlite:///./var/cmep.db"))


DATABASE_URL = _db_url()
ASYNC_DATABASE_URL = DATABASE_URL
SYNC_DATABASE_URL = DATABASE_URL.replace("+aiosqlite", "").replace("+asyncpg", "")

connect_args = {"check_same_thread": False} if DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite") else {}

engine: AsyncEngine = create_async_engine(
    DATABASE_URL,
    echo=bool(getattr(getattr(settings, "database", None), "echo", False)),
    pool_pre_ping=True,
    connect_args=connect_args,
)

async_engine = engine

async_session_maker = async_sessionmaker(
    bind=engine,
    class_=AsyncSession,
    expire_on_commit=False,
    autoflush=False,
)

AsyncSessionLocal = async_session_maker
async_session_factory = async_session_maker
SessionLocal = async_session_maker


async def get_db_session() -> AsyncGenerator[AsyncSession, None]:
    async with async_session_maker() as session:
        try:
            yield session
            await session.commit()
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
            raise


get_session = get_db_session
get_db = get_db_session


async def dispose_engine() -> None:
    await engine.dispose()


dispose = dispose_engine
close_engine = dispose_engine


async def get_engine() -> AsyncEngine:
    return engine
get_async_session = get_db_session

--- seed_database.py ---
import asyncio
from sqlalchemy import select
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import engine, SessionLocal
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.register_models import *
from app.modules.users.infrastructure.models import UserModel
from app.shared.application.passwords import PasswordService
from datetime import datetime, timezone

async def init():
    print("   [DB] Vytváram tabuľky...")
    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
    print("   [DB] Tabuľky hotové")

    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        result = await session.execute(
            select(UserModel).where(UserModel.email == "admin@cmep.dev")
        )
        if result.scalar_one_or_none():
            print("   [DB] Admin už existuje")
            return

        ps = PasswordService()
        admin = UserModel(
            id="00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
            status="active",
            email="admin@cmep.dev",
            password_hash=ps.hash_password("Admin123!"),
            first_name="System",
            last_name="Admin",
            display_name="Administrator",
            created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
        session.add(admin)
        await session.commit()
        print("   [DB] Admin vytvorený: admin@cmep.dev / Admin123!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(init())

===== 10 APPLICATION BOOTSTRAP =====
--- app/main.py ---
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.http_exception_handlers import register_http_exception_handlers

logger = logging.getLogger("cmep.main")


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    logger.info(
        "Starting CMEP app env=%s docs=%s db=%s",
        settings.app_env,
        settings.ENABLE_PUBLIC_DOCS,
        settings.DATABASE_URL,
    )
    yield
    logger.info("Stopping CMEP app")


def _cors_origins() -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(settings.CORS_ORIGINS, list):
        return settings.CORS_ORIGINS
    if isinstance(settings.CORS_ORIGINS, str) and settings.CORS_ORIGINS.strip():
        return [settings.CORS_ORIGINS.strip()]
    return ["http://127.0.0.1:3001", "http://localhost:3001"]


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(
        title=settings.app_name,
        description=settings.app_description,
        version=settings.app_version,
        docs_url="/docs" if settings.ENABLE_PUBLIC_DOCS else None,
        redoc_url="/redoc" if settings.ENABLE_PUBLIC_DOCS else None,
        openapi_url="/openapi.json" if settings.ENABLE_PUBLIC_DOCS else None,
        root_path=settings.ROOT_PATH or "",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    register_http_exception_handlers(app)

    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=_cors_origins(),
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    mounted: list[str] = []
    skipped: list[dict[str, str]] = []

    @app.get("/health")
    async def health():
        return {
            "status": "ok",
            "service": settings.app_name,
            "version": settings.app_version,
            "env": settings.app_env,
            "database_url": settings.DATABASE_URL,
            "mounted_routers": mounted,
            "skipped_routers": skipped,
        }

    try:
        from app.shared.presentation.api_router import api_router

        pref = getattr(api_router, "prefix", "") or ""
        if pref.startswith("/api"):
            app.include_router(api_router)
        else:
            app.include_router(api_router, prefix="/api/v1")
        mounted.append("shared.api_router")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("Failed to include shared.api_router")
        skipped.append({"module": "shared.api_router", "error": str(e)})

    module_routers = [
        "app.modules.auth.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.users.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.companies.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.listings.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.inquiries.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.documents.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.compliance.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.notifications.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.analytics.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.admin.presentation.router",
        "app.modules.uploads.presentation.router",
    ]

    for mod_path in module_routers:
        tag = mod_path.split(".")[2]
        try:
            mod = importlib.import_module(mod_path)
            router = getattr(mod, "router", None)
            if router is None:
                skipped.append({"module": tag, "error": "router attribute missing"})
                logger.warning("Router missing for module=%s", tag)
                continue

            app.include_router(router, prefix="/api/v1")
            mounted.append(tag)
        except Exception as ex:
            logger.exception("Failed to include router module=%s", tag)
            skipped.append({"module": tag, "error": str(ex)})

    app.state.mounted_routers = mounted
    app.state.skipped_routers = skipped

    logger.info("Mounted routers=%s skipped=%s", mounted, skipped)

    return app


app = create_app()

--- app/core/config.py ---
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import List, Union

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


SQLITE_DEFAULT = "sqlite+aiosqlite:///./var/cmep.db"


class JWTSettings(BaseModel):
    secret_key: str = "cmep-dev-change-me-to-at-least-32-chars-long-secret"
    algorithm: str = "HS256"
    access_token_expire_minutes: int = 60
    refresh_token_expire_days: int = 14
    secret: str = ""
    expire_minutes: int = 60

    def model_post_init(self, __context) -> None:
        if not self.secret:
            object.__setattr__(self, "secret", self.secret_key)


class RedisSettings(BaseModel):
    url: str = "memory://"
    host: str = "localhost"
    port: int = 6379
    db: int = 0
    enabled: bool = False


class RateLimitSettings(BaseModel):
    enabled: bool = False
    limit: int = 10
    period: int = 60
    requests: int = 100
    window_seconds: int = 60


class DatabaseSettings(BaseModel):
    url: str = SQLITE_DEFAULT
    async_url: str = SQLITE_DEFAULT
    echo: bool = False
    pool_size: int = 5
    pool_pre_ping: bool = True

    def model_post_init(self, __context) -> None:
        if not self.async_url:
            object.__setattr__(self, "async_url", self.url)


def _is_placeholder_db_value(raw: str | None) -> bool:
    value = str(raw or "").strip()
    return (
        not value
        or "{" in value
        or "}" in value
        or "POSTGRES_" in value
        or value.lower() in {"none", "null", "undefined"}
    )


def _normalize_database_url(raw: str) -> str:
    value = raw.strip()

    if value.startswith("postgresql://"):
        return value.replace("postgresql://", "postgresql+asyncpg://", 1)

    if value.startswith("postgres://"):
        return value.replace("postgres://", "postgresql+asyncpg://", 1)

    if value.startswith("sqlite:///"):
        return value.replace("sqlite:///", "sqlite+aiosqlite:///", 1)

    return value


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        extra="ignore",
        case_sensitive=False,
        populate_by_name=True,
    )

    app_name: str = "CMEP Platform"
    app_version: str = "0.1.0"
    app_description: str = "CMEP - Platform for circular materials exchange"

    app_env: str = Field(default="development", alias="APP_ENV")
    DEBUG: bool = True
    ENABLE_PUBLIC_DOCS: bool = True
    ROOT_PATH: str = ""

    HOST: str = "0.0.0.0"
    PORT: int = 8001

    CORS_ORIGINS: Union[List[str], str] = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: [
            "http://127.0.0.1:3001",
            "http://localhost:3001",
            "http://localhost:3000",
            "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
        ]
    )

    DATABASE_URL: str = SQLITE_DEFAULT

    JWT_SECRET_KEY: str = "cmep-dev-change-me-to-at-least-32-chars-long-secret"
    JWT_ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 60
    REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS: int = 14

    jwt: JWTSettings | None = None
    redis: RedisSettings = Field(default_factory=RedisSettings)
    rate_limit: RateLimitSettings = Field(default_factory=RateLimitSettings)
    database: DatabaseSettings | None = None

    GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: str = ""
    GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET: str = ""
    GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI: str = "http://127.0.0.1:3001/api/auth/callback/google"
    NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8001"
    NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: str = ""

    @property
    def is_development(self) -> bool:
        return self.app_env.lower() in {"development", "dev", "local", "test"}

    @field_validator("CORS_ORIGINS", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def parse_cors(cls, value):
        if value is None or value == "":
            return ["http://127.0.0.1:3001", "http://localhost:3001"]
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return value

        text = str(value).strip()
        if text.startswith("["):
            return json.loads(text)

        return [item.strip() for item in text.split(",") if item.strip()]

    def model_post_init(self, __context) -> None:
        raw_db = str(self.DATABASE_URL or "").strip()

        if _is_placeholder_db_value(raw_db):
            if self.is_development:
                database_url = SQLITE_DEFAULT
            else:
                raise ValueError("DATABASE_URL is missing or invalid for non-development environment.")
        else:
            database_url = _normalize_database_url(raw_db)

        object.__setattr__(self, "DATABASE_URL", database_url)
        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "database",
            DatabaseSettings(
                url=database_url,
                async_url=database_url,
            ),
        )

        if self.jwt is None:
            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "jwt",
                JWTSettings(
                    secret_key=self.JWT_SECRET_KEY,
                    secret=self.JWT_SECRET_KEY,
                    algorithm=self.JWT_ALGORITHM,
                    access_token_expire_minutes=self.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES,
                    expire_minutes=self.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES,
                    refresh_token_expire_days=self.REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS,
                ),
            )


settings = Settings()

--- app/core/http_exception_handlers.py ---
from fastapi import Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

from app.shared.domain.exceptions import (
    NotFoundDomainError,
    UnauthorizedDomainError,
    ValidationDomainError,
)


def register_http_exception_handlers(app):
    @app.exception_handler(UnauthorizedDomainError)
    async def unauthorized_domain_handler(request: Request, exc: UnauthorizedDomainError):
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=401,
            content={"detail": str(exc) or "Unauthorized"},
        )

    @app.exception_handler(NotFoundDomainError)
    async def not_found_domain_handler(request: Request, exc: NotFoundDomainError):
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=404,
            content={"detail": str(exc) or "Not found"},
        )

    @app.exception_handler(ValidationDomainError)
    async def validation_domain_handler(request: Request, exc: ValidationDomainError):
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=422,
            content={"detail": str(exc) or "Validation error"},
        )

--- app/shared/presentation/auth_dependencies.py ---
from fastapi import Depends
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer

from app.modules.auth.application.services import GetCurrentAuthContextService
from app.modules.auth.presentation.dependencies import get_auth_service
from app.modules.auth.presentation.schemas import CurrentUserResponse

bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


async def require_current_user(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    service: GetCurrentAuthContextService = Depends(get_auth_service),
) -> CurrentUserResponse:
    if credentials is None:
        from app.shared.domain.exceptions import UnauthorizedDomainError
        raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Authentication required")
    return await service.execute(credentials.credentials)


def _user_email(current_user) -> str:
    u = getattr(current_user, "user", None)
    if u is not None:
        return (getattr(u, "email", None) or "").lower().strip()
    return (getattr(current_user, "email", None) or "").lower().strip()


def _role_codes(current_user) -> list[str]:
    codes = getattr(current_user, "roleCodes", None) or getattr(current_user, "role_codes", None) or []
    return [str(c).lower() for c in codes]


async def require_admin(current_user: CurrentUserResponse = Depends(require_current_user)) -> CurrentUserResponse:
    """Platform admin: email in PLATFORM_ADMIN_EMAILS or role code admin."""
    from app.core.config import settings
    from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ForbiddenDomainError

    email = _user_email(current_user)
    raw = getattr(settings, "platform_admin_emails", None)
    if raw is None:
        import os
        raw = os.getenv("PLATFORM_ADMIN_EMAILS", "admin@cmep-bootstrap.dev")
    admins = {e.strip().lower() for e in str(raw).split(",") if e.strip()}
    codes = _role_codes(current_user)
    if email in admins or "admin" in codes or "platform_admin" in codes:
        return current_user
    raise ForbiddenDomainError("Admin access required")

--- app/modules/auth/application/password_reset.py ---
"""Password reset via Redis token + optional SMTP."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import logging
import secrets
from uuid import UUID

from app.core.config import settings
from app.modules.users.domain.repositories import UserRepository
from app.shared.application.passwords import PasswordService
from app.shared.infrastructure.email.sender import send_email
from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_service import JwtService

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

RESET_PREFIX = "pwdreset:"
RESET_TTL_SECONDS = 60 * 30


def _token_digest(token: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


class PasswordResetService:
    _mem: dict = {}

    def __init__(
        self,
        user_repo: UserRepository,
        password_service: PasswordService,
        jwt_service: JwtService,
        redis_client=None,
    ):
        self.user_repo = user_repo
        self.password_service = password_service
        self.jwt_service = jwt_service
        self.redis = redis_client

    def _get_redis(self):
        if self.redis is not None:
            return self.redis
        try:
            import redis

            host = getattr(settings.redis, "host", "localhost")
            port = getattr(settings.redis, "port", 6379)
            url = getattr(settings.redis, "url", None) or f"redis://{host}:{port}/0"
            self.redis = redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True)
            self.redis.ping()
            return self.redis
        except Exception:
            self.redis = None
            return None

    def _store_token(self, token: str, user_id: str) -> None:
        from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError

        key = RESET_PREFIX + _token_digest(token)
        r = self._get_redis()
        if r is not None:
            r.set(key, user_id, ex=RESET_TTL_SECONDS)
            return

        if settings.is_development:
            logger.warning("Password reset using in-memory fallback storage")
            PasswordResetService._mem[key] = user_id
            return

        logger.error("Password reset unavailable: Redis is not reachable")
        raise ValidationDomainError(
            "Obnova hesla je dočasne nedostupná. Skúste to neskôr."
        )

    def _pop_token(self, token: str) -> str | None:
        key = RESET_PREFIX + _token_digest(token)
        r = self._get_redis()
        if r is not None:
            uid = r.get(key)
            if uid:
                r.delete(key)
            return uid

        if settings.is_development:
            return PasswordResetService._mem.pop(key, None)

        return None

    async def request_reset(self, email: str) -> dict:
        email_n = email.lower().strip()
        user = await self.user_repo.get_by_email(email_n)
        result = {
            "ok": True,
            "message": "Ak účet existuje, poslali sme inštrukcie na e-mail.",
        }
        if user is None:
            return result

        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        self._store_token(token, str(user.id))

        base = settings.frontend_url.rstrip("/")
        nl = chr(10)
        body = (
            "Obnova hesla CMEP"
            + nl + nl
            + f"Token (platný 30 min): {token}"
            + nl + nl
            + f"Link: {base}/reset-password?token={token}"
            + nl
        )
        sent = send_email(to=email_n, subject="CMEP – obnova hesla", body=body)
        result["emailSent"] = bool(sent)
        if settings.is_development:
            result["resetToken"] = token
        return result

    async def reset_password(self, token: str, new_password: str) -> None:
        from app.shared.domain.exceptions import (
            NotFoundDomainError,
            ValidationDomainError,
        )

        if not token or len(new_password) < 8:
            raise ValidationDomainError(
                "Neplatný token alebo heslo (min. 8 znakov)."
            )
        user_id = self._pop_token(token)
        if not user_id:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Token je neplatný alebo expirovaný.")
        user = await self.user_repo.get_by_id(UUID(str(user_id)))
        if user is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("User not found")
        user.password_hash = self.password_service.hash_password(new_password)
        await self.user_repo.update(user)
        await self.jwt_service.revoke_all_user_tokens(str(user.id))

--- app/modules/auth/application/services.py ---
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

from app.modules.auth.application.dto import AuthTokenPairDTO
from app.modules.users.infrastructure.repositories import SqlAlchemyUserRepository
from app.shared.application.passwords import PasswordService
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import UnauthorizedDomainError
from app.shared.domain.ports.clock import Clock
from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_exceptions import (
    InvalidTokenError,
    TokenExpiredError,
    TokenFamilyRevokedError,
    TokenRevokedError,
    TokenUserRevokedError,
)
from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_service import JwtService

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class LoginService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        jwt_service: JwtService,
        user_repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository,
        password_service: PasswordService,
    ):
        self.jwt_service = jwt_service
        self.user_repo = user_repo
        self.password_service = password_service

    async def execute(self, email: str, password: str) -> AuthTokenPairDTO:
        user = await self.user_repo.get_by_email(email)
        if not user or not self.password_service.verify_password(
            password, user.password_hash
        ):
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Invalid credentials")

        subject = str(user.id)
        family_id = str(uuid4())
        access_token, access_expires_at = await self.jwt_service.issue_access_token(
            subject=subject
        )
        refresh_token, refresh_jti, refresh_expires_at = (
            await self.jwt_service.issue_refresh_token(
                subject=subject,
                family_id=family_id,
            )
        )

        expires_in = int(
            (access_expires_at - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()
        )

        return AuthTokenPairDTO(
            access_token=access_token,
            refresh_token=refresh_token,
            token_type="Bearer",
            expires_in=expires_in,
        )


class RefreshTokenService:
    def __init__(self, jwt_service: JwtService, clock: Clock):
        self.jwt_service = jwt_service
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(self, refresh_token: str) -> AuthTokenPairDTO:
        try:
            payload = await self.jwt_service.decode_and_validate(
                refresh_token, expected_type="refresh"
            )
        except TokenFamilyRevokedError as e:
            logger.warning(f"Replay attack detected: {e}")
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError(
                "Session invalidated due to security concern"
            )
        except TokenUserRevokedError:
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Session has been revoked")

        sub = payload.get("sub")
        family_id = payload.get("family_id")
        old_jti = payload.get("jti")
        old_exp = datetime.fromtimestamp(payload.get("exp"), tz=timezone.utc)

        if await self.jwt_service.revocation_store.is_family_revoked(family_id):
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Session has been revoked")

        await self.jwt_service.revoke_refresh_token(old_jti, family_id, old_exp)

        access_token, access_expires_at = await self.jwt_service.issue_access_token(
            subject=sub
        )
        new_refresh_token, new_jti, refresh_expires_at = (
            await self.jwt_service.issue_refresh_token(
                subject=sub,
                family_id=family_id,
            )
        )

        expires_in = int(
            (access_expires_at - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds()
        )

        return AuthTokenPairDTO(
            access_token=access_token,
            refresh_token=new_refresh_token,
            token_type="Bearer",
            expires_in=expires_in,
        )


class GetCurrentAuthContextService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        jwt_service: JwtService,
        user_repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository,
        membership_repo,
    ):
        self.jwt_service = jwt_service
        self.user_repo = user_repo
        self.membership_repo = membership_repo

    async def execute(self, access_token: str):
        from uuid import UUID

        from app.modules.auth.application.dto import CurrentAuthContextDTO
        from app.modules.users.application.mappers import to_user_dto

        try:
            payload = await self.jwt_service.decode_and_validate(
                access_token, expected_type="access"
            )
        except TokenExpiredError:
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Token has expired")
        except TokenRevokedError:
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Token has been revoked")
        except TokenUserRevokedError:
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("Session has been revoked")
        except InvalidTokenError as e:
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError(f"Invalid token: {e.message}")

        sub = payload.get("sub")
        user = await self.user_repo.get_by_id(UUID(sub))
        if user is None:
            raise UnauthorizedDomainError("User not found")

        memberships = await self.membership_repo.list_by_user_id(UUID(sub))

        role_codes = sorted({m.membership_role.value for m in memberships})

        membership_dicts = [
            {
                "id": m.id,
                "companyId": m.company_id,
                "membershipRole": m.membership_role.value,
                "status": m.status.value,
                "isPrimaryContact": m.is_primary_contact,
            }
            for m in memberships
        ]

        return CurrentAuthContextDTO(
            user=to_user_dto(user),
            role_codes=role_codes,
            memberships=membership_dicts,
        )


class LogoutService:
    def __init__(self, jwt_service: JwtService):
        self.jwt_service = jwt_service

    async def execute(self, refresh_token: str | None = None) -> None:
        if refresh_token:
            try:
                refresh_payload = await self.jwt_service.decode_and_validate(
                    refresh_token, expected_type="refresh"
                )
                refresh_jti = refresh_payload.get("jti")
                refresh_family = refresh_payload.get("family_id")
                refresh_exp = datetime.fromtimestamp(
                    refresh_payload.get("exp"), tz=timezone.utc
                )
                await self.jwt_service.revoke_refresh_token(
                    refresh_jti, refresh_family, refresh_exp
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"Could not revoke refresh token: {e}")

        logger.info("User logged out successfully")

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID, uuid4


@dataclass(slots=True)
class RefreshSession:
    user_id: UUID
    token_family_id: str
    refresh_token_jti: str
    refresh_token_hash: str
    expires_at: datetime

    revoked_at: datetime | None = None
    replaced_by_jti: str | None = None
    reuse_detected_at: datetime | None = None

    id: UUID = field(default_factory=uuid4)
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)
    created_by_user_id: UUID | None = None
    updated_by_user_id: UUID | None = None

--- app/modules/auth/domain/repositories.py ---
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.auth.domain.entities import RefreshSession


class RefreshSessionRepository(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    async def add(self, session: RefreshSession) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_jti(self, jti: str) -> RefreshSession | None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_active_by_user_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> list[RefreshSession]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, session: RefreshSession) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def revoke_family(self, family_id: str, revoked_at) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.auth.domain.entities import RefreshSession
from app.modules.auth.infrastructure.models import RefreshSessionModel


def to_domain(model: RefreshSessionModel) -> RefreshSession:
    return RefreshSession(
        id=UUID(model.id),
        user_id=UUID(model.user_id),
        token_family_id=model.token_family_id,
        refresh_token_jti=model.refresh_token_jti,
        refresh_token_hash=model.refresh_token_hash,
        expires_at=model.expires_at,
        revoked_at=model.revoked_at,
        replaced_by_jti=model.replaced_by_jti,
        reuse_detected_at=model.reuse_detected_at,
        created_at=model.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=UUID(model.created_by_user_id) if model.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=model.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=UUID(model.updated_by_user_id) if model.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )


def to_model(entity: RefreshSession) -> RefreshSessionModel:
    return RefreshSessionModel(
        id=str(entity.id),
        user_id=str(entity.user_id),
        token_family_id=entity.token_family_id,
        refresh_token_jti=entity.refresh_token_jti,
        refresh_token_hash=entity.refresh_token_hash,
        expires_at=entity.expires_at,
        revoked_at=entity.revoked_at,
        replaced_by_jti=entity.replaced_by_jti,
        reuse_detected_at=entity.reuse_detected_at,
        created_at=entity.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=str(entity.created_by_user_id) if entity.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=entity.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=str(entity.updated_by_user_id) if entity.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )

--- app/modules/auth/infrastructure/models.py ---
from sqlalchemy import DateTime, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class RefreshSessionModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "refresh_sessions"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)
    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False, index=True)
    token_family_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False, index=True)
    refresh_token_jti: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False, unique=True, index=True)
    refresh_token_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    expires_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    revoked_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    replaced_by_jti: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    reuse_detected_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    created_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)

--- app/modules/auth/infrastructure/repositories.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.auth.domain.entities import RefreshSession
from app.modules.auth.domain.repositories import RefreshSessionRepository
from app.modules.auth.infrastructure.mappers import to_domain, to_model
from app.modules.auth.infrastructure.models import RefreshSessionModel


class SqlAlchemyRefreshSessionRepository(RefreshSessionRepository):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def add(self, session_entity: RefreshSession) -> None:
        self.session.add(to_model(session_entity))
        await self.session.commit()

    async def get_by_jti(self, jti: str) -> RefreshSession | None:
        stmt = select(RefreshSessionModel).where(RefreshSessionModel.refresh_token_jti == jti)
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def get_active_by_user_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> list[RefreshSession]:
        stmt = select(RefreshSessionModel).where(
            RefreshSessionModel.user_id == str(user_id),
            RefreshSessionModel.revoked_at.is_(None),
        )
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return [to_domain(item) for item in result.scalars().all()]

    async def update(self, session_entity: RefreshSession) -> None:
        model = await self.session.get(RefreshSessionModel, str(session_entity.id))
        if model is None:
            return
        model.revoked_at = session_entity.revoked_at
        model.replaced_by_jti = session_entity.replaced_by_jti
        model.reuse_detected_at = session_entity.reuse_detected_at
        model.updated_at = session_entity.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = str(session_entity.updated_by_user_id) if session_entity.updated_by_user_id else None
        await self.session.commit()

    async def revoke_family(self, family_id: str, revoked_at) -> None:
        stmt = (
            update(RefreshSessionModel)
            .where(RefreshSessionModel.token_family_id == family_id, RefreshSessionModel.revoked_at.is_(None))
            .values(revoked_at=revoked_at, updated_at=revoked_at)
        )
        await self.session.execute(stmt)
        await self.session.commit()

from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_revocation_store import get_revocation_store
from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_service import JwtService


def get_jwt_service() -> JwtService:
    revocation_store = get_revocation_store()
    return JwtService(revocation_store=revocation_store)


def get_password_service() -> PasswordService:
    return PasswordService()


def get_auth_service(
    jwt_service: Annotated[JwtService, Depends(get_jwt_service)],
    session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db_session)],
) -> GetCurrentAuthContextService:
    user_repo = SqlAlchemyUserRepository(session=session)
    membership_repo = SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository(session=session)
    return GetCurrentAuthContextService(
        jwt_service=jwt_service,
        user_repo=user_repo,
        membership_repo=membership_repo,
    )


def get_login_service(
    jwt_service: Annotated[JwtService, Depends(get_jwt_service)],
    password_service: Annotated[PasswordService, Depends(get_password_service)],
    session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db_session)],
) -> LoginService:
    user_repo = SqlAlchemyUserRepository(session=session)
    return LoginService(
        jwt_service=jwt_service,
        user_repo=user_repo,
        password_service=password_service,
    )


def get_password_reset_service(
    jwt_service: Annotated[JwtService, Depends(get_jwt_service)],
    password_service: Annotated[PasswordService, Depends(get_password_service)],
    session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db_session)],
) -> PasswordResetService:
    user_repo = SqlAlchemyUserRepository(session=session)
    return PasswordResetService(
        user_repo=user_repo,
        password_service=password_service,
        jwt_service=jwt_service,
    )


def get_refresh_token_service(
    jwt_service: Annotated[JwtService, Depends(get_jwt_service)],
) -> RefreshTokenService:
    clock = SystemClock()
    return RefreshTokenService(jwt_service=jwt_service, clock=clock)


def get_logout_service(
    jwt_service: Annotated[JwtService, Depends(get_jwt_service)],
) -> LogoutService:
    return LogoutService(jwt_service=jwt_service)


def get_refresh_token_service_alias():
    return get_refresh_token_service

--- app/modules/auth/presentation/router.py ---
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request, Response, status

from app.modules.auth.presentation.dependencies import (
    get_auth_service,
    get_login_service,
    get_logout_service,
    get_password_reset_service,
    get_refresh_token_service,
)
from app.modules.auth.presentation.schemas import (
    AuthTokenPairResponse,
    CurrentUserResponse,
    ForgotPasswordRequest,
    LoginRequest,
    LogoutRequest,
    MembershipContext,
    RefreshTokenRequest,
    ResetPasswordRequest,
)
from app.modules.users.presentation.mappers import to_user_data
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError, ValidationDomainError
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["auth"])


@router.post(
    "/login",
    response_model=AuthTokenPairResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 422, 500),
)
async def login(request: Request, payload: LoginRequest, service=Depends(get_login_service)):
    from app.shared.infrastructure.rate_limit import check_rate_limit, client_ip
    ip = client_ip(request)
    check_rate_limit(f"login:{ip}", limit=10, period=60)
    check_rate_limit(f"login:email:{payload.email.lower().strip()}", limit=5, period=60)
    dto = await service.execute(email=payload.email, password=payload.password)
    return AuthTokenPairResponse(
        accessToken=dto.access_token,
        refreshToken=dto.refresh_token,
        tokenType=dto.token_type,
        expiresIn=dto.expires_in,
    )


@router.post(
    "/refresh",
    response_model=AuthTokenPairResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 422, 500),
)
async def refresh_token(payload: RefreshTokenRequest, service=Depends(get_refresh_token_service)):
    dto = await service.execute(refresh_token=payload.refreshToken)
    return AuthTokenPairResponse(
        accessToken=dto.access_token,
        refreshToken=dto.refresh_token,
        tokenType=dto.token_type,
        expiresIn=dto.expires_in,
    )


@router.post(
    "/logout",
    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,
    responses=error_responses(401, 422, 500),
)
async def logout(payload: LogoutRequest, service=Depends(get_logout_service)):
    await service.execute(refresh_token=payload.refreshToken)
    return Response(status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)


@router.get(
    "/me",
    response_model=CurrentUserResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 500),
)
async def me(current_user=Depends(require_current_user), service=Depends(get_auth_service)):
    context = current_user
    return CurrentUserResponse(
        user=to_user_data(context.user),
        roleCodes=context.role_codes,
        memberships=[
            MembershipContext(
                id=item["id"],
                companyId=item["companyId"],
                membershipRole=item["membershipRole"],
                status=item["status"],
                isPrimaryContact=item["isPrimaryContact"],
            )
            for item in context.memberships
        ],
    )


@router.post(
    "/forgot-password",
    responses=error_responses(400, 422, 500),
)
async def forgot_password(
    request: Request,
    payload: ForgotPasswordRequest,
    svc=Depends(get_password_reset_service),
):
    from app.shared.infrastructure.rate_limit import check_rate_limit, client_ip

    ip = client_ip(request)
    check_rate_limit(f"forgot:{ip}", limit=5, period=60)
    check_rate_limit(f"forgot:email:{payload.email.lower().strip()}", limit=3, period=300)

    return await svc.request_reset(payload.email)


@router.post(
    "/reset-password",
    responses=error_responses(400, 404, 422, 500),
)
async def reset_password(
    payload: ResetPasswordRequest,
    svc=Depends(get_password_reset_service),
):
    await svc.reset_password(payload.token, payload.newPassword)
    return {"ok": True, "message": "Heslo bolo zmenené."}

from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr

from app.modules.users.presentation.schemas import UserData


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str


class RefreshTokenRequest(BaseModel):
    refreshToken: str


class LogoutRequest(BaseModel):
    refreshToken: str | None = None


class AuthTokenPairResponse(BaseModel):
    accessToken: str
    refreshToken: str
    tokenType: str
    expiresIn: int


class MembershipContext(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    companyId: UUID
    membershipRole: str
    status: str
    isPrimaryContact: bool


class CurrentUserResponse(BaseModel):
    user: UserData
    roleCodes: list[str]
    memberships: list[MembershipContext]

class ForgotPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    email: str


class ResetPasswordRequest(BaseModel):
    token: str
    newPassword: str

from passlib.context import CryptContext

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")


class PasswordService:
    def hash_password(self, raw_password: str) -> str:
        return pwd_context.hash(raw_password)

    def verify_password(self, raw_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
        return pwd_context.verify(raw_password, hashed_password)

--- app/shared/application/side_effects.py ---
"""Audit + notifications – best-effort."""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _uuid(v):
    if v is None:
        return None
    return v if isinstance(v, UUID) else UUID(str(v))


async def write_audit(*, session, actor_user_id, action: str, target_type: str, target_id=None, details: str | None = None):
    try:
        if not actor_user_id:
            return
        from app.modules.admin.domain.entities import AdminAuditEntryEntity
        from app.modules.admin.domain.enums import AdminAuditEntryStatus
        from app.modules.admin.infrastructure.repositories import AdminAuditEntryRepositoryImpl

        now = datetime.utcnow()
        aid = _uuid(actor_user_id)
        entity = AdminAuditEntryEntity(
            id=uuid4(),
            actor_user_id=aid,
            action=action,
            target_type=target_type,
            target_id=_uuid(target_id),
            details=details,
            status=AdminAuditEntryStatus.SUCCESS,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
            created_by_user_id=aid,
            updated_by_user_id=None,
        )
        await AdminAuditEntryRepositoryImpl(session).create(entity)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("write_audit failed: %s", action)


async def notify_company_members(
    *,
    session,
    company_id,
    title: str,
    message: str,
    notif_type: str = "inquiry_received",
    related_entity_id=None,
    exclude_user_id=None,
):
    try:
        if not company_id:
            return
        from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.repositories import (
            SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository,
        )
        from app.modules.notifications.domain.entities import NotificationEntity
        from app.modules.notifications.infrastructure.repositories import NotificationRepositoryImpl

        cid = _uuid(company_id)
        members = await SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository(session).list_by_company_id(cid)
        nrepo = NotificationRepositoryImpl(session)
        now = datetime.utcnow()
        rid = _uuid(related_entity_id)
        excl = str(exclude_user_id) if exclude_user_id else None
        for m in members:
            uid = getattr(m, "user_id", None)
            if uid is None or (excl and str(uid) == excl):
                continue
            await nrepo.create(
                NotificationEntity(
                    id=uuid4(),
                    user_id=_uuid(uid),
                    type=notif_type,
                    title=title,
                    message=message[:2000],
                    is_read=False,
                    related_entity_id=rid,
                    created_at=now,
                    updated_at=now,
                    created_by_user_id=None,
                    updated_by_user_id=None,
                )
            )
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("notify_company_members failed")

--- app/shared/infrastructure/security/jwt_exceptions.py ---
from typing import Any


class JwtServiceError(Exception):
    """Base exception for JWT service errors."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, details: dict[str, Any] | None = None):
        self.message = message
        self.details = details or {}
        super().__init__(self.message)


class TokenExpiredError(JwtServiceError):
    """Token has expired."""
    pass


class TokenRevokedError(JwtServiceError):
    """Token has been revoked."""
    pass


class TokenFamilyRevokedError(JwtServiceError):
    """Token family has been revoked (replay attack detected)."""
    pass


class TokenUserRevokedError(JwtServiceError):
    """All user sessions issued before cutoff have been revoked."""
    pass


class InvalidTokenError(JwtServiceError):
    """Token is malformed or signature is invalid."""
    pass


class TokenTypeMismatchError(JwtServiceError):
    """Token type doesn't match expected type."""
    pass

--- app/shared/infrastructure/security/jwt_revocation_store.py ---
"""Token revocation store — Redis or in-memory (Termux / dev)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any

from app.core.config import settings

REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS = int(str(settings.jwt.refresh_token_expire_days))


class _MemoryBackend:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._store: dict[str, tuple[str, float | None]] = {}

    def _purge(self) -> None:
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
        dead = [k for k, (_, exp) in self._store.items() if exp is not None and exp <= now]
        for k in dead:
            self._store.pop(k, None)

    async def set(self, key: str, value: str, ex: int | None = None) -> None:
        self._purge()
        exp = None
        if ex is not None and ex > 0:
            exp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() + ex
        self._store[key] = (value, exp)

    async def get(self, key: str) -> str | None:
        self._purge()
        item = self._store.get(key)
        if not item:
            return None
        return item[0]

    async def exists(self, key: str) -> int:
        self._purge()
        return 1 if key in self._store else 0

    async def close(self) -> None:
        self._store.clear()


class TokenRevocationStore:
    def __init__(self, redis_url: str | None = None):
        self.redis_url = redis_url or str(settings.redis.url)
        self._client: Any = None
        self._use_memory = (
            not getattr(settings.redis, "enabled", False)
            or self.redis_url.startswith("memory:")
            or self.redis_url in ("", "none", "disabled")
        )

    async def _get_client(self) -> Any:
        if self._client is not None:
            return self._client
        if self._use_memory:
            self._client = _MemoryBackend()
            return self._client
        try:
            import redis.asyncio as redis
            self._client = redis.from_url(self.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
            return self._client
        except Exception:
            self._client = _MemoryBackend()
            self._use_memory = True
            return self._client

    async def revoke_access_token(self, jti: str, exp: datetime) -> None:
        client = await self._get_client()
        ttl = int((exp - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds())
        if ttl > 0:
            await client.set(f"revoked:access:{jti}", "1", ex=ttl)

    async def revoke_refresh_token(self, jti: str, family_id: str, exp: datetime) -> None:
        client = await self._get_client()
        ttl = int((exp - datetime.now(timezone.utc)).total_seconds())
        if ttl > 0:
            await client.set(f"revoked:refresh:{jti}", "1", ex=ttl)
            await client.set(f"revoked:family:{family_id}", "1", ex=ttl)

    async def revoke_user_tokens_after(self, subject: str, cutoff: datetime) -> None:
        client = await self._get_client()
        cutoff_utc = cutoff.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        ttl = int(timedelta(days=REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS).total_seconds())
        await client.set(
            f"revoked:user:{subject}:after",
            cutoff_utc.isoformat(),
            ex=ttl,
        )

    async def get_user_tokens_revoked_after(self, subject: str) -> datetime | None:
        client = await self._get_client()
        value = await client.get(f"revoked:user:{subject}:after")
        if not value:
            return None
        try:
            return datetime.fromisoformat(value)
        except Exception:
            return None

    async def is_access_token_revoked(self, jti: str) -> bool:
        client = await self._get_client()
        try:
            return await client.exists(f"revoked:access:{jti}") > 0
        except Exception:
            return False

    async def is_family_revoked(self, family_id: str) -> bool:
        client = await self._get_client()
        try:
            return await client.exists(f"revoked:family:{family_id}") > 0
        except Exception:
            return False

    async def close(self) -> None:
        if self._client is not None:
            close = getattr(self._client, "close", None)
            if close:
                await close()
            self._client = None


_revocation_store: TokenRevocationStore | None = None


def get_revocation_store() -> TokenRevocationStore:
    global _revocation_store
    if _revocation_store is None:
        _revocation_store = TokenRevocationStore()
    return _revocation_store

--- app/shared/infrastructure/security/jwt_service.py ---
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

import jwt

from app.core.config import settings
from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_exceptions import (
    InvalidTokenError,
    TokenExpiredError,
    TokenFamilyRevokedError,
    TokenRevokedError,
    TokenTypeMismatchError,
    TokenUserRevokedError,
)
from app.shared.infrastructure.security.jwt_revocation_store import (
    TokenRevocationStore,
    get_revocation_store,
)

JWT_SECRET = settings.jwt.secret_key
JWT_ALGORITHM = settings.jwt.algorithm
ACCESS_TOKEN_TTL_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN_TTL_MINUTES", "15"))
REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS = int(str(settings.jwt.refresh_token_expire_days))


class JwtService:
    def __init__(self, revocation_store: TokenRevocationStore | None = None):
        self._revocation_store = revocation_store
        self._owns_store = revocation_store is None

    @property
    def revocation_store(self) -> TokenRevocationStore:
        if self._revocation_store is None:
            self._revocation_store = get_revocation_store()
        return self._revocation_store

    async def issue_access_token(
        self, subject: str, extra_claims: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    ) -> tuple[str, datetime]:
        issued_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        expires_at = issued_at + timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_TTL_MINUTES)
        jti = str(uuid4())
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "sub": subject,
            "type": "access",
            "jti": jti,
            "iat": issued_at,
            "exp": expires_at,
        }
        if extra_claims:
            payload.update(extra_claims)
        return jwt.encode(payload, JWT_SECRET, algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM), expires_at

    async def issue_refresh_token(
        self, subject: str, family_id: str, extra_claims: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    ) -> tuple[str, str, datetime]:
        issued_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        expires_at = issued_at + timedelta(days=REFRESH_TOKEN_TTL_DAYS)
        jti = str(uuid4())
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "sub": subject,
            "type": "refresh",
            "jti": jti,
            "family_id": family_id,
            "iat": issued_at,
            "exp": expires_at,
        }
        if extra_claims:
            payload.update(extra_claims)
        return jwt.encode(payload, JWT_SECRET, algorithm=JWT_ALGORITHM), jti, expires_at

    async def decode_and_validate(
        self, token: str, expected_type: str = "access"
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Decode and validate token with full security checks.
        - Verifies signature
        - Checks expiry
        - Checks revocation list
        - Validates token type
        """
        try:
            payload = jwt.decode(token, JWT_SECRET, algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM])
        except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
            raise TokenExpiredError("Token has expired")
        except jwt.InvalidTokenError as e:
            raise InvalidTokenError(f"Invalid token: {str(e)}")

        token_type = payload.get("type")
        if token_type != expected_type:
            raise TokenTypeMismatchError(
                f"Expected {expected_type} token, got {token_type}"
            )

        sub = payload.get("sub")
        iat_raw = payload.get("iat")
        if sub and iat_raw is not None:
            token_issued_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(iat_raw, tz=timezone.utc)
            revoked_after = await self.revocation_store.get_user_tokens_revoked_after(sub)
            if revoked_after is not None and token_issued_at <= revoked_after:
                raise TokenUserRevokedError(
                    "All existing user sessions have been revoked"
                )

        if expected_type == "access":
            jti = payload.get("jti")
            if await self.revocation_store.is_access_token_revoked(jti):
                raise TokenRevokedError("Access token has been revoked")

        if expected_type == "refresh":
            family_id = payload.get("family_id")
            if await self.revocation_store.is_family_revoked(family_id):
                raise TokenFamilyRevokedError(
                    "Token family revoked - possible replay attack"
                )

        return payload

    async def revoke_access_token(self, jti: str, exp: datetime) -> None:
        await self.revocation_store.revoke_access_token(jti, exp)

    async def revoke_refresh_token(self, jti: str, family_id: str, exp: datetime) -> None:
        await self.revocation_store.revoke_refresh_token(jti, family_id, exp)

    async def revoke_all_user_tokens(
        self, subject: str, cutoff: datetime | None = None
    ) -> None:
        await self.revocation_store.revoke_user_tokens_after(
            subject,
            cutoff or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

    def decode(self, token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return jwt.decode(token, JWT_SECRET, algorithms=[JWT_ALGORITHM])

"""admin application services."""

from typing import List, Optional

from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError

from ..domain.entities import AdminAuditEntryEntity
from ..domain.repositories import AdminAuditEntryRepository


class AdminAuditEntryService:
    """AdminAuditEntry service."""

    def __init__(self, repository: AdminAuditEntryRepository):
        self.repository = repository

    async def get_by_id(self, id: str) -> Optional[AdminAuditEntryEntity]:
        return await self.repository.get_by_id(id)

    async def list_all(self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20) -> List[AdminAuditEntryEntity]:
        return await self.repository.list_all(page, limit)

    async def list_by_actor(self, actor_user_id, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20):
        return await self.repository.list_by_actor(actor_user_id, page, limit)

    async def count_by_actor(self, actor_user_id) -> int:
        return await self.repository.count_by_actor(actor_user_id)

    async def count_all(self) -> int:
        return await self.repository.count_all()

    async def create(self, entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
        entity.validate()
        return await self.repository.create(entity)

    async def update(self, id: str, entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"AdminAuditEntry {id} not found")
        entity.id = existing.id
        entity.created_at = existing.created_at
        entity.created_by_user_id = existing.created_by_user_id
        entity.validate()
        return await self.repository.update(entity)

    async def delete(self, id: str) -> None:
        existing = await self.repository.get_by_id(id)
        if existing is None:
            raise NotFoundDomainError(f"AdminAuditEntry {id} not found")
        await self.repository.delete(id)

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.shared.domain.base import AuditFields
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError


@dataclass(slots=True)
class AdminAuditEntryEntity(AuditFields):
    """AdminAuditEntry entity."""

    actor_user_id: UUID
    action: str
    target_type: str
    status: AdminAuditEntryStatus = AdminAuditEntryStatus.SUCCESS
    target_id: UUID | None = None
    details: str | None = None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not self.action:
            raise ValidationDomainError("AdminAuditEntry action is required")
        if not self.target_type:
            raise ValidationDomainError("AdminAuditEntry target_type is required")

--- app/modules/admin/domain/repositories.py ---
"""admin domain repositories."""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import List, Optional

from .entities import AdminAuditEntryEntity


class AdminAuditEntryRepository(ABC):
    """AdminAuditEntry repository interface."""

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_id(self, id: str) -> Optional[AdminAuditEntryEntity]:
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_all(self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20) -> List[AdminAuditEntryEntity]:
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    async def count_all(self) -> int:
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    async def create(self, entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    async def delete(self, id: str) -> None:
        pass

from app.modules.admin.domain.enums import AdminAuditEntryStatus

def to_domain(model: AdminAuditEntryModel) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
    return AdminAuditEntryEntity(
        id=model.id,
        actor_user_id=model.actor_user_id,
        action=model.action,
        target_type=model.target_type,
        target_id=model.target_id,
        details=model.details,
        status=AdminAuditEntryStatus(model.status),
        created_at=model.created_at,
        updated_at=model.updated_at,
        created_by_user_id=model.created_by_user_id,
        updated_by_user_id=model.updated_by_user_id,
    )

        action=entity.action,

--- app/modules/admin/infrastructure/models.py ---
"""admin database models."""

import uuid
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Column, DateTime, String, Text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class AdminAuditEntryModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "admin_audit_entries"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    actor_user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=False)
    action = Column(String(120), nullable=False)
    target_type = Column(String(120), nullable=False)
    target_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=True)
    details = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    status = Column(String(32), nullable=False, default="success")
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)
    created_by_user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=True)
    updated_by_user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=True)

--- app/modules/admin/infrastructure/repositories.py ---
"""admin repository implementations."""

from typing import List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import delete as sql_delete
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from ..domain.entities import AdminAuditEntryEntity
from ..domain.repositories import AdminAuditEntryRepository
from .mappers import to_domain, to_model
from .models import AdminAuditEntryModel


class AdminAuditEntryRepositoryImpl(AdminAuditEntryRepository):
    """AdminAuditEntry repository implementation."""

    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def get_by_id(self, id: str) -> Optional[AdminAuditEntryEntity]:
        result = await self.session.execute(select(AdminAuditEntryModel).where(AdminAuditEntryModel.id == id))
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_by_actor(self, actor_user_id, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20):
        offset = (page - 1) * limit
        result = await self.session.execute(
            select(AdminAuditEntryModel)
            .where(AdminAuditEntryModel.actor_user_id == actor_user_id)
            .order_by(AdminAuditEntryModel.created_at.desc())
            .offset(offset)
            .limit(limit)
        )
        return [to_domain(m) for m in result.scalars().all()]

    async def count_by_actor(self, actor_user_id) -> int:
        result = await self.session.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(AdminAuditEntryModel).where(AdminAuditEntryModel.actor_user_id == actor_user_id)
        )
        return int(result.scalar_one())

    async def list_all(self, page: int = 1, limit: int = 20) -> List[AdminAuditEntryEntity]:
        offset = (page - 1) * limit
        result = await self.session.execute(select(AdminAuditEntryModel).order_by(AdminAuditEntryModel.created_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit))
        models = result.scalars().all()
        return [to_domain(m) for m in models]

    async def count_all(self) -> int:
        result = await self.session.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(AdminAuditEntryModel))
        return int(result.scalar_one())

    async def create(self, entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
        model = to_model(entity)
        self.session.add(model)
        await self.session.commit()
        await self.session.refresh(model)
        return to_domain(model)

    async def update(self, entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryEntity:
        model = await self.session.get(AdminAuditEntryModel, entity.id)
        if model is None:
            raise ValueError("Entity not found")
        model.actor_user_id = entity.actor_user_id
        model.action = entity.action
        model.target_type = entity.target_type
        model.target_id = entity.target_id
        model.details = entity.details
        model.status = entity.status.value
        model.updated_at = entity.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = entity.updated_by_user_id
        await self.session.commit()
        await self.session.refresh(model)
        return to_domain(model)

    async def delete(self, id: str) -> None:
        await self.session.execute(sql_delete(AdminAuditEntryModel).where(AdminAuditEntryModel.id == id))
        await self.session.commit()

--- app/modules/admin/presentation/router.py ---
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_admin
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
"""admin API router."""

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status as http_status

from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError

from ..application.services import AdminAuditEntryService
from ..domain.entities import AdminAuditEntryEntity
from .dependencies import get_admin_service
from .schemas import (
    AdminAuditEntryCreateRequest,
    AdminAuditEntryData,
    AdminAuditEntryResponse,
    PaginatedAdminAuditEntriesResponse,
    PaginationMeta,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["admin-audit"])


def _to_data(entity: AdminAuditEntryEntity) -> AdminAuditEntryData:
    return AdminAuditEntryData(
        id=entity.id,
        actorUserId=entity.actor_user_id,
        action=entity.action,
        targetType=entity.target_type,
        targetId=entity.target_id,
        details=entity.details,
        status=entity.status.value,
        createdAt=entity.created_at,
        updatedAt=entity.updated_at,
    )


@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=PaginatedAdminAuditEntriesResponse,
    summary="List current user's audit entries",
    operation_id="listCurrentUserAuditEntries",
)
async def list_admin(
    page: int = 1,
    limit: int = 20,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service: AdminAuditEntryService = Depends(get_admin_service),
):
    uid = getattr(getattr(current_user, "user", None), "id", None) or getattr(current_user, "id", None)
    items = await service.list_by_actor(uid, page, limit)
    total = await service.count_by_actor(uid)
    return PaginatedAdminAuditEntriesResponse(
        items=[_to_data(e) for e in items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=limit, total=total),
    )


@router.get(
    "/all",
    response_model=PaginatedAdminAuditEntriesResponse,
    summary="List all audit entries",
    operation_id="listAllAuditEntries",
)
async def list_all_admin(
    page: int = 1,
    limit: int = 20,
    current_user=Depends(require_admin),
    service: AdminAuditEntryService = Depends(get_admin_service),
):
    items = await service.list_all(page, limit)
    total = await service.count_all()
    return PaginatedAdminAuditEntriesResponse(
        items=[_to_data(e) for e in items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=limit, total=total),
    )


@router.get(
    "/{id}",
    response_model=AdminAuditEntryResponse,
    summary="Get audit entry by ID",
    operation_id="getAuditEntryById",
)
async def get_admin(id: str, service: AdminAuditEntryService = Depends(get_admin_service)):
    entity = await service.get_by_id(id)
    if entity is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=http_status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="AdminAuditEntry not found")
    return AdminAuditEntryResponse(data=_to_data(entity))


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=AdminAuditEntryResponse,
    status_code=http_status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Create audit entry",
    operation_id="createAuditEntry",
)
async def create_admin(
    request: AdminAuditEntryCreateRequest,
    service: AdminAuditEntryService = Depends(get_admin_service),
):
    entity = AdminAuditEntryEntity(
        actor_user_id=request.actorUserId,
        action=request.action,
        target_type=request.targetType,
        target_id=request.targetId,
        details=request.details,
    )
    try:
        created = await service.create(entity)
    except ValidationDomainError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=http_status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, detail=exc.message) from exc
    return AdminAuditEntryResponse(data=_to_data(created))

    action: str | None = None
    targetType: str | None = None
    targetId: UUID | None = None

    status: str | None = None

    action: str
    targetType: str

    details: str | None
    status: str
    createdAt: datetime
    updatedAt: datetime

    pageSize: int

--- app/modules/admin/service.py ---
"""
Legacy compatibility service for admin module.

The authoritative implementation lives in the layered application/domain/
infrastructure packages. This placeholder keeps old imports from crashing
while callers are migrated to the new dependencies.
"""

from typing import List, Optional


class AdminService:
    def __init__(self):
        self._items: List[dict] = []

    def get_all(self) -> List[dict]:
        return self._items

    def get_by_id(self, id: int) -> Optional[dict]:
        for item in self._items:
            if item.get("id") == id:
                return item
        return None

    def create(self, data: dict) -> dict:
        new_id = len(self._items) + 1
        item = {"id": new_id, **data}
        self._items.append(item)
        return item

    def update(self, id: int, data: dict) -> Optional[dict]:
        for i, item in enumerate(self._items):
            if item.get("id") == id:
                updated = {**item, **data}
                self._items[i] = updated
                return updated
        return None

    def delete(self, id: int) -> bool:
        for i, item in enumerate(self._items):
            if item.get("id") == id:
                del self._items[i]
                return True
        return False

--- app/modules/company_memberships/application/services.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.application.dto import CompanyMembershipDTO
from app.modules.company_memberships.application.mappers import to_membership_dto
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import CompanyMembershipRole, CompanyMembershipStatus
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.repositories import CompanyMembershipRepository
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import NotFoundDomainError


class CreateCompanyMembershipService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository, uuid_generator: UuidGenerator, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.uuid_generator = uuid_generator
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        company_id: UUID,
        user_id: UUID,
        membership_role: str,
        is_primary_contact: bool,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> CompanyMembershipDTO:
        now = self.clock.now()
        membership = CompanyMembership(
            id=self.uuid_generator.new(),
            status=CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE,
            company_id=company_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            membership_role=CompanyMembershipRole(membership_role),
            is_primary_contact=is_primary_contact,
            invited_at=now,
            joined_at=now,
            left_at=None,
            created_at=now,
            created_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
            updated_at=now,
            updated_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
        )
        membership.validate()
        await self.repo.add(membership)
        return to_membership_dto(membership)


class ListCompanyMembershipsService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository):
        self.repo = repo

    async def execute(self, *, company_id: UUID, status: str | None) -> list[CompanyMembershipDTO]:
        items = await self.repo.list_by_company_id(company_id, status)
        return [to_membership_dto(item) for item in items]


class UpdateCompanyMembershipService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        membership_id: UUID,
        membership_role: str | None,
        is_primary_contact: bool | None,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> CompanyMembershipDTO:
        membership = await self.repo.get_by_id(membership_id)
        if not membership:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company membership not found")
        if membership_role is not None:
            membership.membership_role = CompanyMembershipRole(membership_role)
        if is_primary_contact is not None:
            membership.is_primary_contact = is_primary_contact
        membership.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        membership.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        await self.repo.update(membership)
        return to_membership_dto(membership)


class TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService:
    def __init__(self, repo: CompanyMembershipRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(self, *, membership_id: UUID, to_status: str, actor_user_id: UUID | None) -> CompanyMembershipDTO:
        membership = await self.repo.get_by_id(membership_id)
        if not membership:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("Company membership not found")
        membership.transition_status(CompanyMembershipStatus(to_status))
        membership.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        membership.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        await self.repo.update(membership)
        return to_membership_dto(membership)

--- app/modules/company_memberships/domain/__init__.py ---

--- app/modules/company_memberships/domain/entities.py ---
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import CompanyMembershipRole, CompanyMembershipStatus
from app.shared.domain.base import AuditFields
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError
from app.shared.domain.status_transition import ensure_transition


@dataclass(slots=True, kw_only=True)
class CompanyMembership(AuditFields):
    status: CompanyMembershipStatus
    company_id: UUID
    user_id: UUID
    membership_role: CompanyMembershipRole
    is_primary_contact: bool
    invited_at: datetime | None = None
    joined_at: datetime | None = None
    left_at: datetime | None = None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not self.company_id:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Membership company_id is required")
        if not self.user_id:
            raise ValidationDomainError("Membership user_id is required")

    def transition_status(self, to_status: CompanyMembershipStatus) -> None:
        allowed = {
            CompanyMembershipStatus.INVITED: {CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE, CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED},
            CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE: {CompanyMembershipStatus.INACTIVE, CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED},
            CompanyMembershipStatus.INACTIVE: {CompanyMembershipStatus.ACTIVE, CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED},
            CompanyMembershipStatus.REMOVED: set(),
        }
        ensure_transition(self.status.value, to_status.value, {s.value for s in allowed[self.status]})
        self.status = to_status

--- app/modules/company_memberships/domain/enums.py ---
from enum import StrEnum


class CompanyMembershipStatus(StrEnum):
    INVITED = "invited"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    INACTIVE = "inactive"
    REMOVED = "removed"


class CompanyMembershipRole(StrEnum):
    OWNER = "owner"
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MEMBER = "member"
    COMPLIANCE_CONTACT = "compliance_contact"
    SALES_CONTACT = "sales_contact"
    LOGISTICS_CONTACT = "logistics_contact"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

--- app/modules/company_memberships/domain/repositories.py ---
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership


class CompanyMembershipRepository(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    async def add(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_id(self, membership_id: UUID) -> CompanyMembership | None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_by_company_id(self, company_id: UUID, status: str | None = None) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def list_by_user_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError
