--- app/modules/company_memberships/infrastructure/models.py ---
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, String
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class CompanyMembershipModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "company_memberships"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    company_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False, index=True)
    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False, index=True)
    membership_role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    is_primary_contact: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    invited_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    joined_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    left_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    created_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)

--- app/modules/company_memberships/infrastructure/repositories.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.entities import CompanyMembership
from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.repositories import CompanyMembershipRepository
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.mappers import to_domain, to_model
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.models import CompanyMembershipModel


class SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository(CompanyMembershipRepository):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def add(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        self.session.add(to_model(membership))
        await self.session.commit()

    async def get_by_id(self, membership_id: UUID) -> CompanyMembership | None:
        stmt = select(CompanyMembershipModel).where(CompanyMembershipModel.id == str(membership_id))
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_by_company_id(self, company_id: UUID, status: str | None = None) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        stmt = select(CompanyMembershipModel).where(CompanyMembershipModel.company_id == str(company_id))
        if status:
            stmt = stmt.where(CompanyMembershipModel.status == status)
        stmt = stmt.order_by(CompanyMembershipModel.created_at.desc())
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return [to_domain(item) for item in result.scalars().all()]

    async def list_by_user_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> list[CompanyMembership]:
        stmt = select(CompanyMembershipModel).where(CompanyMembershipModel.user_id == str(user_id))
        stmt = stmt.order_by(CompanyMembershipModel.created_at.desc())
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        return [to_domain(item) for item in result.scalars().all()]

    async def update(self, membership: CompanyMembership) -> None:
        model = await self.session.get(CompanyMembershipModel, str(membership.id))
        if model is None:
            return
        model.status = membership.status.value
        model.membership_role = membership.membership_role.value
        model.is_primary_contact = membership.is_primary_contact
        model.invited_at = membership.invited_at
        model.joined_at = membership.joined_at
        model.left_at = membership.left_at
        model.updated_at = membership.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = str(membership.updated_by_user_id) if membership.updated_by_user_id else None
        await self.session.commit()

from fastapi import Depends
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.company_memberships.application.services import (
    CreateCompanyMembershipService,

    TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService,
    UpdateCompanyMembershipService,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.infrastructure.repositories import SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session


def get_membership_repo(session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session)) -> SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository:
    return SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository(session)


def get_create_membership_service(repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo)) -> CreateCompanyMembershipService:
    return CreateCompanyMembershipService(repo=repo, uuid_generator=UuidGenerator(), clock=Clock())


def get_list_memberships_service(repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo)) -> ListCompanyMembershipsService:
    return ListCompanyMembershipsService(repo=repo)


def get_update_membership_service(repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo)) -> UpdateCompanyMembershipService:
    return UpdateCompanyMembershipService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())


def get_transition_membership_status_service(repo: SqlAlchemyCompanyMembershipRepository = Depends(get_membership_repo)) -> TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService:
    return TransitionCompanyMembershipStatusService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())

--- app/modules/company_memberships/presentation/router.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.dependencies import (
    get_create_membership_service,
    get_list_memberships_service,
    get_transition_membership_status_service,
    get_update_membership_service,
)
from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.mappers import to_membership_data
from app.modules.company_memberships.presentation.schemas import (
    CompanyMembershipCreateRequest,
    CompanyMembershipResponse,
    CompanyMembershipStatusTransitionRequest,
    CompanyMembershipUpdateRequest,
    PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse,
    PaginationMeta,
)
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses

router = APIRouter(tags=["company-memberships"])


@router.get(
    "/companies/{company_id}/memberships",
    response_model=PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_company_memberships(
    company_id: UUID,
    status: str | None = None,
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=100, ge=1, le=100),
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_list_memberships_service),
):
    items = await service.execute(company_id=company_id, status=status)
    start = (page - 1) * pageSize
    slice_items = items[start:start + pageSize]
    return PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse(
        items=[to_membership_data(item) for item in slice_items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=pageSize, total=len(items)),
    )


@router.post(
    "/companies/{company_id}/memberships",
    response_model=CompanyMembershipResponse,
    status_code=201,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def create_company_membership(
    company_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyMembershipCreateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_create_membership_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(
        company_id=company_id,
        user_id=payload.userId,
        membership_role=payload.membershipRole,
        is_primary_contact=payload.isPrimaryContact,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyMembershipResponse(data=to_membership_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/company-memberships/{membership_id}",
    response_model=CompanyMembershipResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def update_company_membership(
    membership_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyMembershipUpdateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_update_membership_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(
        membership_id=membership_id,
        membership_role=payload.membershipRole,
        is_primary_contact=payload.isPrimaryContact,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyMembershipResponse(data=to_membership_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/company-memberships/{membership_id}/status",
    response_model=CompanyMembershipResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def transition_company_membership_status(
    membership_id: UUID,
    payload: CompanyMembershipStatusTransitionRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_transition_membership_status_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(
        membership_id=membership_id,
        to_status=payload.toStatus,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return CompanyMembershipResponse(data=to_membership_data(dto))

from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.modules.company_memberships.domain.enums import (
    CompanyMembershipRole,
    CompanyMembershipStatus,
)


class CompanyMembershipCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    userId: UUID
    membershipRole: CompanyMembershipRole
    isPrimaryContact: bool = False


class CompanyMembershipUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    membershipRole: CompanyMembershipRole | None = None
    isPrimaryContact: bool | None = None


class CompanyMembershipStatusTransitionRequest(BaseModel):
    toStatus: CompanyMembershipStatus


class CompanyMembershipData(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    status: CompanyMembershipStatus
    companyId: UUID
    userId: UUID
    membershipRole: CompanyMembershipRole
    isPrimaryContact: bool
    invitedAt: datetime | None
    joinedAt: datetime | None
    leftAt: datetime | None
    createdAt: datetime
    createdByUserId: UUID | None
    updatedAt: datetime
    updatedByUserId: UUID | None


class CompanyMembershipResponse(BaseModel):
    data: CompanyMembershipData


class PaginationMeta(BaseModel):
    page: int
    pageSize: int
    total: int


class PaginatedCompanyMembershipsResponse(BaseModel):
    items: list[CompanyMembershipData]
    meta: PaginationMeta

--- app/modules/users/application/services.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.users.application.dto import UserDTO
from app.modules.users.application.mappers import to_user_dto
from app.modules.users.domain.entities import User
from app.modules.users.domain.enums import UserStatus
from app.modules.users.domain.repositories import UserRepository
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ConflictDomainError, NotFoundDomainError


class CreateUserService:
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository, uuid_generator: UuidGenerator, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.uuid_generator = uuid_generator
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        email: str,
        password_hash: str,
        first_name: str,
        last_name: str,
        display_name: str | None,
        phone: str | None,
        locale: str | None,
        timezone: str | None,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> UserDTO:
        existing = await self.repo.get_by_email(email)
        if existing:
            raise ConflictDomainError("User with this email already exists")
        now = self.clock.now()
        user = User(
            id=self.uuid_generator.new(),
            status=UserStatus.ACTIVE,
            email=email.lower().strip(),
            password_hash=password_hash,
            first_name=first_name.strip(),
            last_name=last_name.strip(),
            display_name=display_name,
            phone=phone,
            locale=locale,
            timezone=timezone,
            last_login_at=None,
            created_at=now,
            created_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
            updated_at=now,
            updated_by_user_id=actor_user_id,
        )
        user.validate()
        await self.repo.add(user)
        return to_user_dto(user)


class GetUserService:
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository):
        self.repo = repo

    async def execute(self, user_id: UUID) -> UserDTO:
        user = await self.repo.get_by_id(user_id)
        if not user:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("User not found")
        return to_user_dto(user)


class ListUsersService:
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository):
        self.repo = repo

    async def execute(self, *, status: str | None, company_id: UUID | None, q: str | None, page: int, page_size: int) -> tuple[list[UserDTO], int]:
        users, total = await self.repo.list(status=status, company_id=company_id, q=q, page=page, page_size=page_size)
        return [to_user_dto(user) for user in users], total


class UpdateUserService:
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
        first_name: str | None,
        last_name: str | None,
        display_name: str | None,
        phone: str | None,
        locale: str | None,
        timezone: str | None,
        actor_user_id: UUID | None,
    ) -> UserDTO:
        user = await self.repo.get_by_id(user_id)
        if not user:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("User not found")
        if first_name is not None:
            user.first_name = first_name
        if last_name is not None:
            user.last_name = last_name
        if display_name is not None:
            user.display_name = display_name
        if phone is not None:
            user.phone = phone
        if locale is not None:
            user.locale = locale
        if timezone is not None:
            user.timezone = timezone
        user.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        user.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        user.validate()
        await self.repo.update(user)
        return to_user_dto(user)


class TransitionUserStatusService:
    def __init__(self, repo: UserRepository, clock: Clock):
        self.repo = repo
        self.clock = clock

    async def execute(self, *, user_id: UUID, to_status: str, actor_user_id: UUID | None) -> UserDTO:
        user = await self.repo.get_by_id(user_id)
        if not user:
            raise NotFoundDomainError("User not found")
        user.transition_status(UserStatus(to_status))
        user.updated_at = self.clock.now()
        user.updated_by_user_id = actor_user_id
        await self.repo.update(user)
        return to_user_dto(user)

--- app/modules/users/domain/__init__.py ---

--- app/modules/users/domain/entities.py ---
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.users.domain.enums import UserStatus
from app.shared.domain.base import AuditFields
from app.shared.domain.exceptions import ValidationDomainError
from app.shared.domain.status_transition import ensure_transition


@dataclass(slots=True)
class User(AuditFields):
    status: UserStatus
    email: str
    password_hash: str
    first_name: str
    last_name: str
    display_name: str | None
    phone: str | None
    locale: str | None
    timezone: str | None
    last_login_at: datetime | None

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if not self.email or "@" not in self.email:
            raise ValidationDomainError("User email must be valid")
        if not self.first_name:
            raise ValidationDomainError("User first_name is required")
        if not self.last_name:
            raise ValidationDomainError("User last_name is required")

    def transition_status(self, to_status: UserStatus) -> None:
        allowed = {
            UserStatus.INVITED: {UserStatus.ACTIVE, UserStatus.DEACTIVATED},
            UserStatus.ACTIVE: {UserStatus.SUSPENDED, UserStatus.DEACTIVATED},
            UserStatus.SUSPENDED: {UserStatus.ACTIVE, UserStatus.DEACTIVATED},
            UserStatus.DEACTIVATED: set(),
        }
        ensure_transition(self.status.value, to_status.value, {s.value for s in allowed[self.status]})
        self.status = to_status

--- app/modules/users/domain/enums.py ---
from enum import StrEnum


class UserStatus(StrEnum):
    INVITED = "invited"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    SUSPENDED = "suspended"
    DEACTIVATED = "deactivated"

--- app/modules/users/domain/repositories.py ---
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.users.domain.entities import User


class UserRepository(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    async def add(self, user: User) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> User | None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def get_by_email(self, email: str) -> User | None:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def list(self, *, status: str | None, company_id: UUID | None, q: str | None, page: int, page_size: int) -> tuple[list[User], int]:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def update(self, user: User) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError

from uuid import UUID

from app.modules.users.domain.entities import User
from app.modules.users.domain.enums import UserStatus
from app.modules.users.infrastructure.models import UserModel


def to_domain(model: UserModel) -> User:
    return User(
        id=UUID(model.id),
        status=UserStatus(model.status),
        email=model.email,
        password_hash=model.password_hash,
        first_name=model.first_name,
        last_name=model.last_name,
        display_name=model.display_name,
        phone=model.phone,
        locale=model.locale,
        timezone=model.timezone,
        last_login_at=model.last_login_at,
        created_at=model.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=UUID(model.created_by_user_id) if model.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=model.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=UUID(model.updated_by_user_id) if model.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )


def to_model(entity: User) -> UserModel:
    return UserModel(
        id=str(entity.id),
        status=entity.status.value,
        email=entity.email,
        password_hash=entity.password_hash,
        first_name=entity.first_name,
        last_name=entity.last_name,
        display_name=entity.display_name,
        phone=entity.phone,
        locale=entity.locale,
        timezone=entity.timezone,
        last_login_at=entity.last_login_at,
        created_at=entity.created_at,
        created_by_user_id=str(entity.created_by_user_id) if entity.created_by_user_id else None,
        updated_at=entity.updated_at,
        updated_by_user_id=str(entity.updated_by_user_id) if entity.updated_by_user_id else None,
    )

from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base


class UserModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)

    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    first_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    last_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    display_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=True)
    phone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    locale: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    timezone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    last_login_at: Mapped[DateTime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    created_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[DateTime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_by_user_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)

--- app/modules/users/infrastructure/repositories.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.users.domain.entities import User
from app.modules.users.domain.repositories import UserRepository
from app.modules.users.infrastructure.mappers import to_domain
from app.modules.users.infrastructure.models import UserModel


class SqlAlchemyUserRepository(UserRepository):
    def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession):
        self.session = session

    async def add(self, user: User) -> None:
        from app.modules.users.infrastructure.mappers import to_model
        self.session.add(to_model(user))
        await self.session.commit()

    async def get_by_id(self, user_id: UUID) -> User | None:
        stmt = select(UserModel).where(UserModel.id == str(user_id))
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def get_by_email(self, email: str) -> User | None:
        stmt = select(UserModel).where(UserModel.email == email.lower().strip())
        result = await self.session.execute(stmt)
        model = result.scalar_one_or_none()
        return to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list(self, *, status: str | None, company_id: UUID | None, q: str | None, page: int, page_size: int) -> tuple[list[User], int]:
        stmt = select(UserModel)
        count_stmt = select(func.count()).select_from(UserModel)

        if status:
            stmt = stmt.where(UserModel.status == status)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(UserModel.status == status)

        if q:
            pattern = f"%{q}%"
            filter_expr = or_(
                UserModel.email.ilike(pattern),
                UserModel.first_name.ilike(pattern),
                UserModel.last_name.ilike(pattern),
            )
            stmt = stmt.where(filter_expr)
            count_stmt = count_stmt.where(filter_expr)

        stmt = stmt.order_by(UserModel.created_at.desc()).offset((page - 1) * page_size).limit(page_size)

        rows = (await self.session.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()
        total = (await self.session.execute(count_stmt)).scalar_one()

        return [to_domain(row) for row in rows], int(total)

    async def update(self, user: User) -> None:
        model = await self.session.get(UserModel, str(user.id))
        if model is None:
            return
        model.password_hash = user.password_hash
        model.status = user.status.value
        model.first_name = user.first_name
        model.last_name = user.last_name
        model.display_name = user.display_name
        model.phone = user.phone
        model.locale = user.locale
        model.timezone = user.timezone
        model.last_login_at = user.last_login_at
        model.updated_at = user.updated_at
        model.updated_by_user_id = str(user.updated_by_user_id) if user.updated_by_user_id else None
        await self.session.commit()

from fastapi import Depends
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.modules.users.application.services import (

    GetUserService,
    ListUsersService,
    TransitionUserStatusService,
    UpdateUserService,
)
from app.modules.users.infrastructure.repositories import SqlAlchemyUserRepository
from app.shared.application.clock import Clock
from app.shared.application.passwords import PasswordService
from app.shared.application.uuid_generator import UuidGenerator
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import get_db_session


def get_user_repo(session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session)) -> SqlAlchemyUserRepository:
    return SqlAlchemyUserRepository(session)


def get_create_user_service(repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository = Depends(get_user_repo)) -> CreateUserService:
    return CreateUserService(repo=repo, uuid_generator=UuidGenerator(), clock=Clock())


def get_get_user_service(repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository = Depends(get_user_repo)) -> GetUserService:
    return GetUserService(repo=repo)


def get_list_users_service(repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository = Depends(get_user_repo)) -> ListUsersService:
    return ListUsersService(repo=repo)


def get_update_user_service(repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository = Depends(get_user_repo)) -> UpdateUserService:
    return UpdateUserService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())


def get_transition_user_status_service(repo: SqlAlchemyUserRepository = Depends(get_user_repo)) -> TransitionUserStatusService:
    return TransitionUserStatusService(repo=repo, clock=Clock())


def get_password_service() -> PasswordService:
    return PasswordService()

--- app/modules/users/presentation/mappers.py ---
from app.modules.users.application.dto import UserDTO
from app.modules.users.presentation.schemas import UserData


def to_user_data(dto: UserDTO) -> UserData:
    return UserData(
        id=dto.id,
        status=dto.status,
        email=dto.email,
        firstName=dto.first_name,
        lastName=dto.last_name,
        displayName=dto.display_name,
        phone=dto.phone,
        locale=dto.locale,
        timezone=dto.timezone,
        lastLoginAt=dto.last_login_at,
        createdAt=dto.created_at,
        createdByUserId=dto.created_by_user_id,
        updatedAt=dto.updated_at,
        updatedByUserId=dto.updated_by_user_id,
    )

--- app/modules/users/presentation/router.py ---
from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from app.modules.users.presentation.dependencies import (
    get_create_user_service,
    get_get_user_service,
    get_list_users_service,
    get_password_service,
    get_transition_user_status_service,
    get_update_user_service,
)
from app.modules.users.presentation.mappers import to_user_data
from app.modules.users.presentation.schemas import (
    PaginatedUsersResponse,
    PaginationMeta,
    UserCreateRequest,
    UserResponse,
    UserStatusTransitionRequest,
    UserUpdateRequest,
)
from app.shared.application.passwords import PasswordService
from app.shared.presentation.auth_dependencies import require_current_user
from app.shared.presentation.error_schemas import error_responses

router = APIRouter(prefix="/users", tags=["users"])


@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=PaginatedUsersResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 422, 500),
)
async def list_users(
    page: int = Query(default=1, ge=1),
    pageSize: int = Query(default=25, ge=1, le=100),
    status: str | None = None,
    companyId: UUID | None = None,
    q: str | None = None,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_list_users_service),
):
    items, total = await service.execute(status=status, company_id=companyId, q=q, page=page, page_size=pageSize)
    return PaginatedUsersResponse(
        items=[to_user_data(item) for item in items],
        meta=PaginationMeta(page=page, pageSize=pageSize, total=total),
    )


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=UserResponse,
    status_code=201,
    responses=error_responses(409, 422, 500),
)
async def create_user(
    payload: UserCreateRequest,
    service=Depends(get_create_user_service),
    password_service: PasswordService = Depends(get_password_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(
        email=payload.email,
        password_hash=password_service.hash_password(payload.password),
        first_name=payload.firstName,
        last_name=payload.lastName,
        display_name=payload.displayName,
        phone=payload.phone,
        locale=payload.locale,
        timezone=payload.timezone,
        actor_user_id=None,
    )
    return UserResponse(data=to_user_data(dto))


@router.get(
    "/{user_id}",
    response_model=UserResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 500),
)
async def get_user(
    user_id: UUID,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_get_user_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(user_id)
    return UserResponse(data=to_user_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/{user_id}",
    response_model=UserResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def update_user(
    user_id: UUID,
    payload: UserUpdateRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_update_user_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(
        user_id=user_id,
        first_name=payload.firstName,
        last_name=payload.lastName,
        display_name=payload.displayName,
        phone=payload.phone,
        locale=payload.locale,
        timezone=payload.timezone,
        actor_user_id=current_user.user.id,
    )
    return UserResponse(data=to_user_data(dto))


@router.patch(
    "/{user_id}/status",
    response_model=UserResponse,
    responses=error_responses(401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500),
)
async def transition_user_status(
    user_id: UUID,
    payload: UserStatusTransitionRequest,
    current_user=Depends(require_current_user),
    service=Depends(get_transition_user_status_service),
):
    dto = await service.execute(user_id=user_id, to_status=payload.toStatus, actor_user_id=current_user.user.id)
    return UserResponse(data=to_user_data(dto))

--- app/modules/users/presentation/schemas.py ---
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr, Field

from app.modules.users.domain.enums import UserStatus


class UserCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    firstName: str
    lastName: str
    password: str = Field(min_length=8)
    displayName: str | None = None
    phone: str | None = None
    locale: str | None = None
    timezone: str | None = None


class UserUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
    firstName: str | None = None
    lastName: str | None = None
    displayName: str | None = None
    phone: str | None = None
    locale: str | None = None
    timezone: str | None = None


class UserStatusTransitionRequest(BaseModel):
    toStatus: UserStatus


class UserData(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    status: UserStatus
    email: EmailStr
    firstName: str
    lastName: str
    displayName: str | None
    phone: str | None
    locale: str | None
    timezone: str | None
    lastLoginAt: datetime | None
    createdAt: datetime
    createdByUserId: UUID | None
    updatedAt: datetime
    updatedByUserId: UUID | None


class UserResponse(BaseModel):
    data: UserData


class PaginationMeta(BaseModel):
    page: int
    pageSize: int
    total: int


class PaginatedUsersResponse(BaseModel):
    items: list[UserData]
    meta: PaginationMeta

--- apps/web/src/app/dashboard/admin/page.tsx ---
'use client';

import { useAuthRequired } from "@/features/auth";
import { useAdminStats } from "@/features/admin";
import { AdminUsersTable } from "@/features/admin";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

export default function AdminPage() {
  const { user, isLoading } = useAuthRequired();
  const { data: stats, isLoading: isLoadingStats } = useAdminStats();
  const safeStats = (stats || {}) as {
    total_users?: number;
    total_listings?: number;
    total_companies?: number;
    pending_verifications?: number;
  };

  if (isLoading) return null;

  // Ochrana: Iba admin môže pristupovať na túto stránku
  if (user && user.role !== 'admin') {
    redirect('/dashboard');
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-8 space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">Administrácia systému</h1>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        <StatCard title="Používatelia" value={safeStats.total_users} loading={isLoadingStats} />
        <StatCard title="Ponuky" value={safeStats.total_listings} loading={isLoadingStats} />
        <StatCard title="Firmy" value={safeStats.total_companies} loading={isLoadingStats} />
        <StatCard title="Čakajúce overenia" value={safeStats.pending_verifications} loading={isLoadingStats} color="text-orange-600" />
      </div>

      <section className="space-y-4">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-800">Správa používateľov</h2>
        <AdminUsersTable />
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatCard({ title, value, loading, color = "text-blue-600" }: { title: string, value?: number, loading: boolean, color?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">{title}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-3xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {loading ? '...' : value ?? 0}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

--- apps/web/src/app/dashboard/listings/[id]/page.tsx ---
'use client';

import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { listingsApi } from '@/features/listings';
import { inquiriesApi } from '@/features/inquiries';
import { useAuthRequired } from '@/features/auth';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';

export default function ListingDetailPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const id = String(params?.id || '');
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user } = useAuthRequired();

  const { data, isLoading, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['listing', id],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res: any = await listingsApi.getListing(id);
      return res?.data ?? res;
    },
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  const [message, setMessage] = useState('Mám záujem o túto ponuku.');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [ok, setOk] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const memberships = (user as any)?.memberships || [];
  const myCompanyId = memberships[0]?.companyId || memberships[0]?.company_id || null;
  const email = (user as any)?.email || (user as any)?.user?.email || '';

  const isOwn =
    data?.companyId &&
    memberships.some(
      (m: any) => String(m.companyId || m.company_id) === String(data.companyId)
    );

  const handleInquiry = async () => {
    if (!myCompanyId) {
      setErr('Najprv si vytvor firmu – dopyt ide v mene tvojej firmy.');
      return;
    }
    if (!message.trim()) {
      setErr('Správa je povinná.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setErr(null);
    try {
      await inquiriesApi.create({
        listingId: id,
        companyId: String(myCompanyId),
        message: message.trim(),
        contactEmail: email || 'unknown@example.com',
        contactPhone: phone || undefined,
      });
      setOk(true);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setErr(e?.message || 'Odoslanie dopytu zlyhalo');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) return <div className="p-8 text-center">Načítavam detail...</div>;
  if (error || !data) {
    return (
      <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-500">
        Ponuku sa nepodarilo načítať.
        <div className="mt-4">
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Späť</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-6">
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>
          ← Späť na dashboard
        </Button>

        <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-2 text-sm text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">{data.status}</div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">{data.title}</h1>
          {data.imageUrl ? (
            <div className="mt-4 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
              <img
                src={data.imageUrl}
                alt={data.title}
                className="max-h-80 w-full object-cover"
              />
            </div>
          ) : null}
          <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600 whitespace-pre-wrap">{data.description || 'Bez popisu'}</p>

          <dl className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
            <div>
              <dt className="text-gray-500">Materiál</dt>
              <dd className="font-medium text-gray-900">{data.materialType}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="text-gray-500">Množstvo</dt>
              <dd className="font-medium text-gray-900">
                {data.quantity} {data.unit}
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="text-gray-500">Cena</dt>
              <dd className="font-medium text-gray-900">
                {data.price != null ? `${data.price} ${data.currency || 'EUR'}` : '—'}
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="text-gray-500">Stav materiálu</dt>
              <dd className="font-medium text-gray-900">{data.condition || '—'}</dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="text-gray-500">Lokalita</dt>
              <dd className="font-medium text-gray-900">{data.location || '—'}</dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
        </div>

        {!isOwn && data.status === 'published' && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Mám záujem</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Pošli dopyt predávajúcej firme. Uvidí správu, e-mail a telefón.
            </p>

            {ok ? (
              <div className="mt-4 rounded bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm text-green-800">
                Dopyt odoslaný. Predávajúci ho uvidí v zozname dopytov.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm text-gray-700">Správa</label>
                  <textarea
                    className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-gray-300 p-2 text-sm"
                    rows={3}
                    value={message}
                    onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm text-gray-700">Telefón (voliteľné)</label>
                  <input
                    className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-gray-300 p-2 text-sm"
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="09xx xxx xxx"
                  />
                </div>
                {err && <div className="text-sm text-red-600">{err}</div>}
                <Button onClick={handleInquiry} disabled={busy}>
                  {busy ? 'Odosielam...' : 'Odoslať dopyt'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {isOwn && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 p-4 text-sm text-blue-800">
            Toto je tvoja ponuka – dopyt tu neodosielaš.
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- apps/web/src/app/forgot-password/page.tsx ---
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { authApi } from '@/features/auth';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [devToken, setDevToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setMsg(null);
    setDevToken(null);
    try {
      const res: any = await authApi.forgotPassword(email);
      setMsg(res.message || 'Ak účet existuje, poslali sme inštrukcie.');
      if (res.resetToken) setDevToken(res.resetToken);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setMsg(err?.message || 'Nepodarilo sa odoslať žiadosť.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gray-50 px-4">
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-4 rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-md">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-center">Zabudnuté heslo</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Zadaj e-mail. Pošleme odkaz na obnovu (v DEV sa token zobrazí tu).</p>
        <Input type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="vas@email.sk" />
        {msg && <div className="rounded bg-blue-50 p-2 text-sm text-blue-800">{msg}</div>}
        {devToken && (
          <div className="rounded bg-amber-50 p-2 text-xs text-amber-900 break-all">
            DEV token: {devToken}
            <div className="mt-2">
              <Link className="text-blue-600 underline" href={`/reset-password?token=${encodeURIComponent(devToken)}`}>
                Pokračovať na nové heslo
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Odosielam...' : 'Odoslať'}</Button>
        <p className="text-center text-sm"><Link href="/login" className="text-blue-600">Späť na prihlásenie</Link></p>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

--- apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx ---
import "@/cmep-dark.css";
import "./globals.css";
import { ReactNode } from "react";

import { AppProviders } from "@/components/providers/app-providers";

export const metadata = {
  title: "CMEP · Obehové materiály",
  description: "Circular Materials Exchange Platform frontend"
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="sk">
      <body>
        <AppProviders>{children}</AppProviders>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

--- apps/web/src/app/reset-password/page.tsx ---
'use client';

import { useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { authApi } from '@/features/auth';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';

function ResetForm() {
  const sp = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [token, setToken] = useState(sp.get('token') || '');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [err, setErr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const onSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setErr(null);
    setMsg(null);
    try {
      const res: any = await authApi.resetPassword(token, password);
      setMsg(res.message || 'Heslo zmenené.');
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/login'), 1200);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setErr(e?.message || 'Obnova zlyhala.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-4 rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-md">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-center">Nové heslo</h1>
      <Input placeholder="Token" value={token} onChange={(e) => setToken(e.target.value)} required />
      <Input type="password" placeholder="Nové heslo (min. 8)" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} minLength={8} required />
      {msg && <div className="rounded bg-green-50 p-2 text-sm text-green-800">{msg}</div>}
      {err && <div className="rounded bg-red-50 p-2 text-sm text-red-700">{err}</div>}
      <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Ukladám...' : 'Zmeniť heslo'}</Button>
      <p className="text-center text-sm"><Link href="/login" className="text-blue-600">Prihlásenie</Link></p>
    </form>
  );
}

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gray-50 px-4">
      <Suspense fallback={<div>Načítavam...</div>}>
        <ResetForm />
      </Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}

--- apps/web/src/features/auth/api.ts ---
import { http } from '../../lib/api/http';
import { AuthResponse, MeResponse } from './types';
import { LoginInput } from './schemas';

export const authApi = {
  login: (data: LoginInput) =>
    http.post<AuthResponse>('/auth/login', data),

  googleLogin: (credential: string) =>
    http.post<AuthResponse>('/auth/google', { credential }),

  logout: (refreshToken: string) =>
    http.post('/auth/logout', { refreshToken }),

  getMe: () =>
    http.get<MeResponse>('/auth/me'),

  forgotPassword: (email: string) =>
    http.post<{ ok: boolean; message: string; resetToken?: string }>(
      '/auth/forgot-password',
      { email }
    ),

  resetPassword: (token: string, newPassword: string) =>
    http.post<{ ok: boolean; message: string }>(
      '/auth/reset-password',
      { token, newPassword }
    ),
};

--- apps/web/src/features/auth/components/login-form.tsx ---
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { loginSchema, LoginInput } from '../schemas';
import { useLogin } from '../hooks/use-login';
import { authApi } from '../api';
import { tokenStore } from '../../../lib/auth/token-store';
import { Button } from '../../../components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '../../../components/ui/input';
import { FormField } from '../../../components/ui/form-field';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

declare global {
  interface Window {
    google?: any;
  }
}

export const LoginForm = () => {
  const { mutate: login, isPending, error } = useLogin();
  const router = useRouter();
  const googleButtonRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<LoginInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema),
  });

  const onSubmit = (data: LoginInput) => {
    login(data, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        router.push('/dashboard');
      },
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const clientId = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID;

    if (!clientId || !googleButtonRef.current) return;

    const initGoogle = () => {
      if (!window.google || !googleButtonRef.current) return;

      googleButtonRef.current.innerHTML = '';

      window.google.accounts.id.initialize({
        client_id: clientId,
        callback: async (response: { credential?: string }) => {
          try {
            if (!response.credential) {
              throw new Error('Google credential missing');
            }

            const result = await authApi.googleLogin(response.credential);
            tokenStore.setTokens(
              result.accessToken,
              result.refreshToken,
              true,
            );
            router.push('/dashboard');
          } catch (e: any) {
            alert(e?.message || 'Google prihlásenie zlyhalo');
          }
        },
      });

      window.google.accounts.id.renderButton(googleButtonRef.current, {
        theme: 'outline',
        size: 'large',
        width: 320,
        text: 'signin_with',
        shape: 'rectangular',
      });
    };

    const existingScript = document.querySelector(
      'script[src="https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client"]'
    ) as HTMLScriptElement | null;

    if (existingScript) {
      if (window.google) initGoogle();
      else existingScript.addEventListener('load', initGoogle, { once: true });
      return;
    }

    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = 'https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client';
    script.async = true;
    script.defer = true;
    script.onload = initGoogle;
    document.head.appendChild(script);

    return () => {
      script.onload = null;
    };
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <form
      onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
      className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-white/60 bg-white/90 p-6 shadow-xl shadow-slate-900/5 backdrop-blur-md sm:p-8"
    >
      <div className="mb-2 text-center">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">Prihlásenie</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Vstup do firemného účtu</p>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {(error as any).message || 'Nesprávne meno alebo heslo'}
        </div>
      )}

      <FormField label="E-mail" error={errors.email?.message}>
        <Input
          type="email"
          placeholder="vas@email.sk"
          {...register('email')}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="rounded-xl"
        />
      </FormField>

      <FormField label="Heslo" error={errors.password?.message}>
        <Input
          type="password"
          placeholder="******"
          {...register('password')}
          disabled={isPending}
          className="rounded-xl"
        />
      </FormField>

      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          defaultChecked
          {...register('remember')}
          className="rounded border-slate-300"
        />
        Zapamätať prihlásenie
      </label>

      <Button
        type="submit"
        className="w-full rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 py-2.5 font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
        disabled={isPending}
      >
        {isPending ? 'Prihlasujem...' : 'Prihlásiť sa'}
      </Button>

      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 pt-1 text-center text-sm">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-emerald-700 hover:underline">
          Zabudnuté heslo?
        </Link>

        <div className="w-full flex justify-center py-1">
          <div ref={googleButtonRef} />
        </div>

        <Link
          href="/register"
          className="font-medium text-slate-700 hover:text-emerald-700 hover:underline"
        >
          Vytvoriť účet
        </Link>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
};

export const listingsApi = {
  getListings: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Listing>>('/listings', { ...params }),

  getMarketplace: (params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Listing>>('/listings/marketplace', { ...params }),

  getListing: (id: string) =>
    http.get<Listing>(`/listings/${id}`),

  createListing: (data: ListingInput) =>
    http.post<Listing>('/listings', data),

  updateListing: (id: string, data: Partial<ListingInput> & { status?: string }) =>
    http.put<Listing>(`/listings/${id}`, data),

  publishListing: (id: string) =>
    http.post<{ data: any }>(`/listings/${id}/publish`, {}),

  deleteListing: (id: string) =>
    http.delete(`/listings/${id}`),

  getMatches: (materialType: string, params?: Record<string, any>) =>
    http.get<PaginatedResponse<Listing>>(`/listings/matches?materialType=${encodeURIComponent(materialType)}`, { ...params }),
};

--- apps/web/src/features/uploads/api.ts ---
import { config } from '../../lib/config';
import { tokenStore } from '../../lib/auth/token-store';

export async function uploadFile(file: File): Promise<{ url: string }> {
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append('file', file);
  const token = tokenStore.getToken();
  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/uploads`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
    body: form,
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(err.detail || err.message || 'Upload failed');
  }
  return res.json();
}

--- ./CONSOLIDATE-SCHEMA-AUTHORITY.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

set -Eeuo pipefail

PROJECT="$HOME/waste-platform"
cd "$PROJECT"

STAMP="$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
BACKUP="$PROJECT/.schema-authority-backup-$STAMP"

echo "============================================================"
echo " WASTE PLATFORM"
echo " SCHEMA / API AUTHORITY CONSOLIDATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo "PROJECT:"
echo "$PROJECT"
echo
echo "BACKUP:"
echo "$BACKUP"
echo

mkdir -p "$BACKUP"

###############################################################################
# 1. PRE-FLIGHT
###############################################################################

echo "============================================================"
echo " 1. PRE-FLIGHT"
echo "============================================================"

command -v python3 >/dev/null
command -v git >/dev/null

git status --short --branch | tee "$BACKUP/git-status-before.txt"

###############################################################################
# 2. BACKUP SOURCE FILES
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 2. SOURCE BACKUP"
echo "============================================================"

mkdir -p "$BACKUP/app"
mkdir -p "$BACKUP/apps"

cp -a app/main.py "$BACKUP/app/main.py" 2>/dev/null || true
cp -a app/shared/infrastructure/db "$BACKUP/app/db" 2>/dev/null || true
cp -a app/core/config.py "$BACKUP/app/config.py" 2>/dev/null || true
cp -a apps/web/src/lib/config.ts "$BACKUP/config.ts" 2>/dev/null || true
cp -a apps/web/.env.local "$BACKUP/web.env.local" 2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 3. REMOVE RUNTIME create_all()
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 3. REMOVE RUNTIME create_all()"
echo "============================================================"

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

files = [
    Path("app/main.py"),
]

for path in files:
    if not path.exists():
        continue

    text = path.read_text()

    old = """    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)

"""

    if old in text:
        text = text.replace(old, "")
        path.write_text(text)
        print(f"[FIXED] {path}: removed Base.metadata.create_all()")
    else:
        print(f"[OK]    {path}: create_all block not found")
PY

###############################################################################
# 4. REMOVE CREATE_ALL FROM ACTIVE DEV FIX SCRIPT
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 4. DEV SCRIPT AUDIT"
echo "============================================================"

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

path = Path("scripts/dev/fix_imports.sh")

if path.exists():
    text = path.read_text()

    old = """    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)

"""

    if old in text:
        text = text.replace(old, "")
        path.write_text(text)
        print("[FIXED] scripts/dev/fix_imports.sh")
    else:
        print("[INFO] create_all block not found")
else:
    print("[INFO] scripts/dev/fix_imports.sh not present")
PY

###############################################################################
# 5. DO NOT TOUCH LEGACY BACKUPS
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 5. LEGACY FILES"
echo "============================================================"

echo
echo "Legacy create_all() references are intentionally NOT modified."
echo "They are historical/dev artifacts and remain outside runtime authority."

###############################################################################
# 6. FRONTEND API AUTHORITY
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"

echo "============================================================"

if [ -f "$CONFIG" ]; then
python3 - <<'PY'

text = path.read_text()

text = text.replace(
    "process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:8001'",
    "process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:8000/api/v1'"
)

path.write_text(text)
print("[FIXED] frontend default API base URL")
PY
fi

###############################################################################
# 7. FIX HARDCODED REGISTER ENDPOINT
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 7. FRONTEND REGISTER ENDPOINT"
echo "============================================================"

REGISTER="apps/web/src/features/auth/hooks/use-register.ts"

if [ -f "$REGISTER" ]; then
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

path = Path("apps/web/src/features/auth/hooks/use-register.ts")
text = path.read_text()

text = text.replace(
    "fetch('http://localhost:8001/api/v1/users'",
    "fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/users`"
)

if "config.apiBaseUrl" in text and "import { config" not in text:
    lines = text.splitlines()

    # Try to add config import after existing imports.
    insert_at = 0
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if line.startswith("import "):
            insert_at = i + 1

    lines.insert(
        insert_at,
        "import { config } from '@/lib/config';"
    )

    text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

path.write_text(text)
print("[FIXED] register endpoint authority")
PY
else
    echo "[INFO] register hook not found"
fi

from pathlib import Path

path = Path("app/shared/infrastructure/db/register_models.py")
text = path.read_text()

required = [
    "AdminAuditEntryModel",
    "AnalyticsEventModel",

    print("[ERROR] Missing model registry entries:")
    for item in missing:
        print(" -", item)
    raise SystemExit(1)

print("[OK] all known SQLAlchemy models registered")
PY

###############################################################################
# 11. CHECK IMPORT ORDER
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 11. MAIN IMPORT AUTHORITY"
echo "============================================================"

sed -n '1,80p' app/main.py

###############################################################################
# 12. UUID / VARCHAR REPORT
###############################################################################

echo

echo " 12. UUID / VARCHAR REPORT"
echo "============================================================"

REPORT="$BACKUP/uuid-varchar-report.txt"

{
    echo "UUID / VARCHAR STATIC REPORT"
    echo "DATE: $(date)"
    echo
    echo "--- String(36) ---"
    grep -RIn \
        --include='*.py' \
        'String(36)' \
        app/modules \
        2>/dev/null || true

    echo
    echo "--- UUID imports ---"
    grep -RIn \
        --include='*.py' \
        'UUID' \
        app/modules \
        2>/dev/null || true
} > "$REPORT"

cat "$REPORT"

###############################################################################
# 13. ACTIVE create_all AUDIT
###############################################################################

echo

echo " 13. ACTIVE create_all AUDIT"
echo "============================================================"

grep -RIn \
    --include='*.py' \
    --include='*.sh' \
    'Base.metadata.create_all' \
    app scripts \
    2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 14. API PORT AUDIT
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 14. API PROCESS AUDIT"
echo "============================================================"

ps -ef | grep -E '[u]vicorn|[p]ython.*app.main' || true

echo
echo "--- PORT 8000 ---"
curl -sS -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' \
    http://127.0.0.1:8000/health \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "--- PORT 8001 ---"
curl -sS -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' \
    http://127.0.0.1:8001/health \
    2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 15. OPENAPI CHECK
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 15. OPENAPI"
echo "============================================================"

for PORT in 8000 8001; do
    echo
    echo "--- $PORT ---"

    curl -sS \
        --max-time 5 \
        "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/openapi.json" \
        -o "$BACKUP/openapi-$PORT.json" \
        2>/dev/null || true

    if [ -s "$BACKUP/openapi-$PORT.json" ]; then
        python3 - "$BACKUP/openapi-$PORT.json" <<'PY'
import json
import sys

path = sys.argv[1]

try:
    data = json.load(open(path))
    print("OPENAPI:", data.get("openapi"))
    print("TITLE:", data.get("info", {}).get("title"))
    print("VERSION:", data.get("info", {}).get("version"))
    print("PATHS:", len(data.get("paths", {})))
except Exception as exc:
    print("OPENAPI PARSE ERROR:", exc)
PY
    else
        echo "OPENAPI unavailable"
    fi
done

###############################################################################
# 16. PYTHON COMPILE CHECK
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 16. PYTHON COMPILE CHECK"
echo "============================================================"

python3 -m compileall -q app
echo "[OK] Python compilation"

###############################################################################
# 17. FRONTEND STATIC API AUDIT
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 17. FRONTEND API REFERENCES"
echo "============================================================"

grep -RIn \
    --exclude-dir=node_modules \
    --exclude-dir=.next \
    --exclude='*.map' \
    -E 'localhost:8000|localhost:8001|NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL' \
    apps/web/src \
    2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 18. GIT DIFF
###############################################################################

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " 18. GIT DIFF"
echo "============================================================"


--- ./FINAL-AUTHORITY-AUDIT.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

set -u

PROJECT="$HOME/waste-platform"
STAMP="$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
REPORT="$PROJECT/FINAL-AUTHORITY-AUDIT-$STAMP.txt"

cd "$PROJECT" || exit 1

exec > >(tee "$REPORT") 2>&1

echo "============================================================"
echo " FINAL SCHEMA / API AUTHORITY AUDIT"
echo "============================================================"
echo "DATE: $(date)"
echo "PROJECT: $PROJECT"
echo
echo "READ-ONLY AUDIT"
echo "NO DATABASE MODIFICATION"
echo "NO ALEMBIC MIGRATION"
echo "NO CREATE_ALL"
echo "NO PROCESS KILL"
echo

###############################################################################
# HELPERS
###############################################################################

section() {
    echo
    echo "============================================================"
    echo " $1"
    echo "============================================================"
}

run_cmd() {
    echo
    echo "+ $*"
    "$@" 2>&1 || true
}

###############################################################################
# 1. PROJECT STATE
###############################################################################

section "1. PROJECT STATE"

run_cmd pwd
run_cmd git status --short --branch
run_cmd git rev-parse --show-toplevel
run_cmd find app -maxdepth 4 -type f \( -name '*.py' -o -name '*.sql' \) | sort | wc -l

###############################################################################
# 2. ORM MODELS
###############################################################################

section "2. ORM MODEL AUTHORITY"

echo "--- infrastructure/models.py ---"

find app/modules \
    -path '*/infrastructure/models.py' \
    -type f \
    -print | sort

echo
echo "--- SQLAlchemy model classes ---"

grep -RInE \
    'class .*Model\(Base\)|Column\(UUID|mapped_column\(.*UUID|mapped_column\(String\(36\)' \
    app/modules/*/infrastructure/models.py \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "--- MODEL REGISTRY ---"

if [ -f app/shared/infrastructure/db/register_models.py ]; then
    sed -n '1,240p' app/shared/infrastructure/db/register_models.py
else
    echo "MISSING: app/shared/infrastructure/db/register_models.py"
fi

###############################################################################
# 3. ALEMBIC
###############################################################################

section "3. ALEMBIC AUTHORITY"

echo "--- alembic.ini ---"

if [ -f alembic.ini ]; then
    grep -nE \
        'sqlalchemy.url|script_location|version_locations' \
        alembic.ini 2>/dev/null || true
else
    echo "MISSING alembic.ini"
fi

echo
echo "--- migration files ---"

find migrations -type f -maxdepth 3 \
    \( -name '*.py' -o -name '*.sql' \) \
    -print 2>/dev/null | sort || true

echo
echo "--- create_table targets ---"

grep -RInE \
    'op\.create_table|op\.drop_table|op\.alter_column|op\.add_column|op\.drop_column' \
    migrations \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "--- Alembic env.py ---"

if [ -f migrations/env.py ]; then
    sed -n '1,260p' migrations/env.py
else
    echo "MISSING migrations/env.py"
fi

echo
echo "--- register_models import inside Alembic ---"

grep -RIn \
    'register_models' \
    migrations app \
    2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 4. CREATE_ALL AUDIT
###############################################################################

section "4. SQLALCHEMY create_all AUDIT"

echo "--- all create_all references ---"

grep -RInE \
    'metadata\.create_all|run_sync\(Base\.metadata\.create_all|Base\.metadata\.create_all' \
    . \
    --exclude-dir=.git \
    --exclude-dir=node_modules \
    --exclude-dir=.next \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "--- context ---"

grep -RInE \
    -C 4 \
    'metadata\.create_all|Base\.metadata\.create_all' \
    app scripts \
    --exclude-dir=node_modules \
    2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 5. DATABASE CONNECTION CONFIG
###############################################################################

section "5. DATABASE CONFIGURATION"

echo "--- environment variable names only ---"

grep -RInE \
    'DATABASE_URL|POSTGRES|PGHOST|PGPORT|PGDATABASE|PGUSER|DB_NAME|DB_HOST' \
    app .env* alembic.ini 2>/dev/null \
    | sed -E \
        's/(PASSWORD|SECRET|TOKEN|KEY)=([^[:space:]]+)/\1=<REDACTED>/g' \
        || true

echo
echo "--- detected PostgreSQL binaries ---"

command -v psql || true
command -v pg_dump || true
command -v alembic || true

###############################################################################
# 6. DATABASE DISCOVERY
###############################################################################

section "6. POSTGRES DATABASE DISCOVERY"

PSQL_BASE=(psql)

if [ -n "${PGHOST:-}" ]; then
    echo "PGHOST is configured"
fi

if [ -n "${PGPORT:-}" ]; then
    echo "PGPORT is configured"
fi

if [ -n "${PGUSER:-}" ]; then
    echo "PGUSER is configured: $PGUSER"
fi

if [ -n "${PGDATABASE:-}" ]; then
    echo "PGDATABASE is configured: $PGDATABASE"
fi

echo
echo "--- PostgreSQL server ---"

psql -Atqc \
    "SELECT version();" \
    2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- databases ---"

psql -l 2>&1 || true

###############################################################################
# 7. RECYCLEHUB SCHEMA
###############################################################################

section "7. DATABASE: recyclehub"

echo "--- tables ---"

psql -d recyclehub -c '\dt public.*' 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- Alembic version ---"

psql -d recyclehub -c \
    'SELECT * FROM public.alembic_version;' \
    2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- table counts ---"

psql -d recyclehub -c "
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    n_live_tup
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename;
" 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- indexes ---"

psql -d recyclehub -c "
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    indexname,
    indexdef
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename, indexname;
" 2>&1 || true

# 8. CMEP SCHEMA
###############################################################################

section "8. DATABASE: cmep"

    'SELECT * FROM public.alembic_version;' \
    2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- table counts ---"

psql -d cmep -c "
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    n_live_tup
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename;
" 2>&1 || true

###############################################################################
# 9. ID TYPE AUDIT
###############################################################################

section "9. DATABASE ID TYPE AUDIT"

for DB in recyclehub cmep; do

    echo
    echo "---------------- DATABASE: $DB ----------------"

    psql -d "$DB" -c "
    SELECT
        table_name,
        column_name,
        data_type,
        udt_name
    FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_schema = 'public'
      AND (
          column_name = 'id'
          OR column_name LIKE '%_id'
      )
    ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position;
    " 2>&1 || true

done

###############################################################################
# 10. FOREIGN KEYS
###############################################################################

section "10. FOREIGN KEY AUDIT"

for DB in recyclehub cmep; do

    echo
    echo "---------------- DATABASE: $DB ----------------"

    psql -d "$DB" -c "
    SELECT
        tc.table_name,
        kcu.column_name,
        ccu.table_name AS foreign_table_name,
        ccu.column_name AS foreign_column_name,
        tc.constraint_name
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints AS tc
    JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage AS kcu
      ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
     AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
    JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage AS ccu
      ON ccu.constraint_name = tc.constraint_name
     AND ccu.table_schema = tc.table_schema
    WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
      AND tc.table_schema = 'public'
    ORDER BY tc.table_name, kcu.column_name;
    " 2>&1 || true

done

###############################################################################
# 11. ORM VS DATABASE TABLE NAMES
###############################################################################

section "11. ORM TABLE NAMES"

grep -RInE \
    '__tablename__\s*=' \
    app/modules/*/infrastructure/models.py \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "--- Database table names: recyclehub ---"

psql -d recyclehub -Atc "
SELECT tablename
FROM pg_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename;
" 2>&1 || true

###############################################################################
# 12. DUPLICATE MODEL FILES
###############################################################################

section "12. DUPLICATE MODEL FILE AUDIT"

find app/modules \
    -type f \
    -name 'models.py' \
    -print | sort

echo
echo "--- model classes ---"

grep -RInE \
    '^class .*Model' \
    app/modules \
    --include='models.py' \
    2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 13. FRONTEND API AUTHORITY
###############################################################################

section "13. FRONTEND API AUTHORITY"

echo "--- config.ts ---"

if [ -f apps/web/src/lib/config.ts ]; then
    cat apps/web/src/lib/config.ts
fi

echo
echo "--- .env.local ---"

if [ -f apps/web/.env.local ]; then
    cat apps/web/.env.local

    echo "MISSING apps/web/.env.local"
fi

echo

grep -RInE \
    'localhost:8000|localhost:8001|NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL|apiBaseUrl' \
    apps/web/src \
    --exclude-dir=node_modules \
    --exclude-dir=.next \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "--- direct hardcoded URLs ---"

grep -RInE \
    'https?://[^"'\'']+' \
    apps/web/src \
    --exclude-dir=node_modules \
    --exclude-dir=.next \
    2>/dev/null || true

#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

set +e

echo "DATE: $(date)"
echo "PROJECT: $PROJECT"
echo

cd "$PROJECT" || exit 1

###############################################################################

###############################################################################

echo "PWD:"
pwd

echo
echo "PYTHON:"
python3 --version 2>&1 || true

echo "ALEMBIC:"
alembic --version 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "PSQL:"
psql --version 2>&1 || true

git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null || true

echo
git status --short 2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 3. ALEMBIC
###############################################################################

echo
echo "================ ALEMBIC CONFIG ===================="

cat alembic.ini 2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "================ ALEMBIC HEADS ===================="

alembic heads 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "================ ALEMBIC HISTORY ===================="

alembic history 2>&1 || true

echo "================ ALEMBIC CURRENT ===================="

alembic current 2>&1 || true

echo "================ ALEMBIC MIGRATION FILES ===================="

find migrations -type f -maxdepth 4 -print 2>/dev/null | sort

echo
echo "================ ALEMBIC TABLE OPERATIONS ===================="

grep -RInE \
'create_table|drop_table|add_column|drop_column|alter_column|create_index|drop_index|create_foreign_key|drop_constraint' \
migrations \
2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 4. REGISTERED ORM MODELS
###############################################################################

echo
echo "================ REGISTERED ORM MODELS ===================="

sed -n '1,300p' app/shared/infrastructure/db/register_models.py 2>/dev/null || true

###############################################################################
# 5. ORM TABLE DECLARATIONS
###############################################################################

echo
echo "================ ORM TABLE DECLARATIONS ===================="

grep -RInE \
'__tablename__|class .*Model\(Base\)' \
app \
--include='*.py' \
2>/dev/null \
| sort || true

###############################################################################
# 6. SQLALCHEMY METADATA
###############################################################################

echo
echo "================ SQLALCHEMY METADATA ===================="

python3 <<'PY'
import sys
import traceback

print("IMPORTING APPLICATION METADATA...")

try:
    from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base

    try:
        import app.shared.infrastructure.db.register_models
        print("MODEL REGISTRATION: OK")
    except Exception as e:
        print("MODEL REGISTRATION ERROR:", repr(e))
        traceback.print_exc()

    tables = sorted(Base.metadata.tables.values(), key=lambda t: t.name)

    print()
    print("TABLE COUNT:", len(tables))

    for table in tables:
        print()
        print("TABLE:", table.name)
        print("COLUMNS:", len(table.columns))
        print("INDEXES:", len(table.indexes))
        print("PRIMARY KEY:", ", ".join(c.name for c in table.primary_key.columns))

        for column in table.columns:
            print(
                "  COLUMN:",
                column.name,
                "| TYPE:", repr(column.type),
                "| NULLABLE:", column.nullable,
                "| DEFAULT:", repr(column.default),
            )

        for index in sorted(table.indexes, key=lambda x: x.name or ""):
            print(
                "  INDEX:",
                index.name,
                "| UNIQUE:", index.unique,
                "| COLUMNS:", ", ".join(c.name for c in index.columns),
            )

except Exception as e:
    print("METADATA ERROR:", repr(e))
    traceback.print_exc()
PY

###############################################################################
# 8. RECYCLEHUB DATABASE
###############################################################################

echo
echo "================ RECYCLEHUB DATABASE ===================="

RECYCLE_USER="recyclehub"
RECYCLE_DB="recyclehub"

echo "DATABASE: $RECYCLE_DB"
echo "USER: $RECYCLE_USER"

echo
echo "--- DATABASE EXISTENCE ---"

psql \
-h 127.0.0.1 \
-p 5432 \
-U "$RECYCLE_USER" \
-d postgres \
-c "SELECT datname FROM pg_database WHERE datname = '$RECYCLE_DB';" \
2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- ALEMBIC VERSION ---"

psql \
-h 127.0.0.1 \
-p 5432 \
-U "$RECYCLE_USER" \
-d "$RECYCLE_DB" \
-c 'SELECT * FROM alembic_version;' \
2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- TABLES ---"

psql \
-h 127.0.0.1 \
-p 5432 \
-U "$RECYCLE_USER" \
-d "$RECYCLE_DB" \
-c '\dt public.*' \
2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- TABLE COLUMNS ---"

psql \
-h 127.0.0.1 \
-p 5432 \
-U "$RECYCLE_USER" \
-d "$RECYCLE_DB" \
-c "
SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    data_type,
    udt_name,
    is_nullable,
    column_default
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'public'
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position;
" \
2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- PRIMARY / UNIQUE / FOREIGN KEY CONSTRAINTS ---"

psql \
-h 127.0.0.1 \
-p 5432 \
-U "$RECYCLE_USER" \
-d "$RECYCLE_DB" \
-c "
SELECT
    tc.table_name,
    tc.constraint_name,
    tc.constraint_type,
    kcu.column_name,
    ccu.table_name AS foreign_table_name,
    ccu.column_name AS foreign_column_name
FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
LEFT JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu
    ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
   AND tc.table_schema = kcu.table_schema
LEFT JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu
    ON tc.constraint_name = ccu.constraint_name
   AND tc.table_schema = ccu.table_schema
WHERE tc.table_schema = 'public'
ORDER BY tc.table_name, tc.constraint_type, tc.constraint_name;
" \
2>&1 || true

echo
echo "--- INDEXES ---"

psql \
-h 127.0.0.1 \
-p 5432 \
-U "$RECYCLE_USER" \
-d "$RECYCLE_DB" \
-c "
SELECT
    schemaname,
    tablename,
    indexname,
    indexdef
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'public'
ORDER BY tablename, indexname;
" \
2>&1 || true

echo

CMEP_USER="cmep"
