    'created_by_user_id',

--- ./Makefile ---
# =============================================================================
# CMEP Platform — Professional Makefile
# =============================================================================

.PHONY: help install install-dev dev dev-worker dev-beat test test-unit test-integration test-cov lint format format-check migrate migrate-create db-upgrade db-downgrade db-reset up down ps logs logs-api monitor-up monitor-down audit audit-security clean clean-all

# --- Default ---
help:	## Zobrazi napovedu
	@grep -E '^[a-zA-Z_-]+:.*?## .*$$' $(MAKEFILE_LIST) | awk 'BEGIN {FS = ":.*?## "}; {printf "\033[36m%-20s\033[0m %s\n", $$1, $$2}'

# --- Environment ---
install:	## Instaluje produkcne zavislosti
	pip install -r requirements.txt

install-dev:	## Instaluje dev zavislosti
	pip install -e ".[dev,test]"

# --- Development ---
dev:	## Spusti backend v dev mode (auto-reload)
	uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload

dev-worker:	## Spusti Celery worker
	celery -A app.worker worker -l info

dev-beat:	## Spusti Celery beat (scheduler)
	celery -A app.worker beat -l info

# --- Testing ---
test:	## Spusti vsetky testy
	pytest app/tests -v

test-unit:	## Spusti unit testy
	pytest app/tests/unit -v -m unit

test-integration:	## Spusti integration testy
	pytest app/tests/integration -v -m integration

test-cov:	## Spusti testy s coverage reportom
	pytest app/tests --cov=app --cov-report=html --cov-report=term

# --- Code Quality ---
lint:	## Spusti linting (flake8 + mypy)
	flake8 app --max-line-length=100 --extend-ignore=E203,W503
	mypy app

format:	## Formatuje kod (black + isort)
	black app
	isort app

format-check:	## Kontrola formatovania bez zmeny
	black --check app
	isort --check-only app

# --- Database ---
migrate:	## Vytvori a aplikuje Alembic migracie
	alembic revision --autogenerate -m "auto_migration"
	alembic upgrade head

migrate-create:	## Vytvori novu migraciu (manualny nazov)
	@read -p "Nazov migracie: " name; \
	alembic revision --autogenerate -m "$$name"

db-upgrade:	## Aplikuje migracie
	alembic upgrade head

db-downgrade:	## Vrati poslednu migraciu
	alembic downgrade -1

db-reset:	## Resetuje databazu (POZOR: maze data!)
	alembic downgrade base
	alembic upgrade head

# --- Docker ---
up:	## Spusti vsetky sluzby v Dockeri
	docker compose up -d --build

down:	## Zastavi Docker sluzby
	docker compose down

ps:	## Stav Docker sluzieb
	docker compose ps

logs:	## Logy zo vsetkych sluzieb
	docker compose logs -f

logs-api:	## Logy iba z API
	docker compose logs -f api

# --- Monitoring ---
monitor-up:	## Spusti monitoring stack
	docker compose -f docker-compose.monitoring.yml up -d

monitor-down:	## Zastavi monitoring stack
	docker compose -f docker-compose.monitoring.yml down

# --- Security Audit ---
audit:	## Forenzny audit projektu
	bash scripts/forensic-audit.sh

audit-security:	## Kontrola hardcoded secretov
	@grep -rn "password\|secret\|token\|api_key" app/ --include="*.py" | grep -v ".pyc" | grep -v "test" || echo "Ziadne obvious secrety v kode (skontroluj .env!)"

# --- Cleanup ---
clean:	## Vycisti cache, pyc, logy
	find . -type d -name "__pycache__" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
	find . -type f -name "*.pyc" -delete 2>/dev/null || true
	find . -type d -name ".mypy_cache" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
	find . -type d -name ".pytest_cache" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
	find . -type d -name ".ruff_cache" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
	rm -rf htmlcov/ .coverage

clean-all: clean	## Vycisti aj Docker volumes (POZOR!)
	docker compose down -v
	docker system prune -f

--- ./app/fix_and_start.sh ---
#!/bin/bash

echo "🔧 OPRAVA A SPUSTENIE APLIKÁCIE"
echo "========================================="

# 1. Choď do koreňa
cd ~/waste-platform
echo "📋 1. Adresár: $(pwd)"

# 2. Skontroluj alembic.ini
echo ""
echo "📋 2. Kontrola alembic.ini..."
if [ ! -f "alembic.ini" ]; then
    echo "⚠️  alembic.ini chýba, vytváram..."
    cat > alembic.ini << 'INIEOF'
[alembic]
script_location = migrations
prepend_sys_path = .
version_path_separator = os
sqlalchemy.url = postgresql+asyncpg://recyclehub:recyclehub@127.0.0.1:5432/recyclehub

[loggers]
keys = root,sqlalchemy,alembic

[handlers]
keys = 

[formatters]
keys = 

[logger_root]
level = WARN
handlers = 
qualname =

[logger_sqlalchemy]
level = WARN
handlers = 
qualname = sqlalchemy.engine

[logger_alembic]
level = INFO
handlers = 
qualname = alembic

[handler_root]
level = INFO
handlers = 

[handler_sqlalchemy]
level = WARN
handlers = 

[handler_alembic]
level = INFO
handlers = 
INIEOF
    echo "✅ alembic.ini vytvorený"
else
    echo "✅ alembic.ini existuje"
fi

# 3. Skontroluj Alembic
echo ""
echo "📋 3. Kontrola Alembic..."
alembic current 2>/dev/null || echo "⚠️  Alembic nie je inicializovaný"

# 4. Zabi staré procesy
echo ""
echo "📋 4. Zastavujem staré procesy..."
pkill -f uvicorn 2>/dev/null
sleep 2

# 5. Spusti aplikáciu
echo ""
echo "📋 5. Spúšťam aplikáciu..."
cd app
uvicorn main:app --reload --port 8000 --host 0.0.0.0 &
sleep 5

# 6. Test
echo ""
echo "📋 6. Test API..."
curl -s http://localhost:8000/health | python3 -m json.tool 2>/dev/null || echo "❌ Aplikácia neodpovedá"

# 7. Zhrnutie
echo ""
echo "========================================="
echo "📊 ZHRNUTIE"
echo "========================================="
echo "✅ Aplikácia: $(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://localhost:8000/health 2>/dev/null)"
echo "✅ Alembic: $(alembic current 2>/dev/null | head -1 || echo 'N/A')"
echo "🔗 http://localhost:8000/docs"
echo "========================================="

#!/bin/bash

echo "🔧 OPRAVA main.py"
echo "========================================="

# 1. Choď do adresára
cd ~/waste-platform/app

# 2. Zálohuj
cp main.py main.py.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)

# 3. Vytvor nový main.py
cat > main.py << 'MAINEOF'
import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from fastapi import FastAPI
from app.core.config import settings
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.session import engine
from app.shared.infrastructure.db import register_models  # noqa: F401

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    logging.info("Starting up...")
    # create_all je zakomentované - používame Alembic migrácie
    # async with engine.begin() as conn:
    #     await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
    yield
    logging.info("Shutting down...")

app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.app_name,
    version="1.0.0",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

@app.get("/health")
async def health():
    return {"status": "ok"}

@app.get("/version")
async def version():
    return {"version": "1.0.0", "app": settings.app_name}
MAINEOF

# 4. Skontroluj syntax
echo ""
echo "📋 Kontrola syntaxe..."
python3 -m py_compile main.py && echo "✅ Syntax OK"

# 5. Zabi staré procesy
echo ""
echo "📋 Zastavujem staré procesy..."
pkill -f uvicorn 2>/dev/null
sleep 2

# 6. Spusti aplikáciu
echo ""
echo "📋 Spúšťam aplikáciu..."
uvicorn main:app --reload --port 8000 --host 0.0.0.0 &

# 7. Počkaj a otestuj
sleep 5
echo ""
echo "📋 Test API..."
curl -s http://localhost:8000/health | python3 -m json.tool

echo ""
echo "========================================="
echo "✅ HOTOVO!"
echo "========================================="

--- ./app/start_app.sh ---
#!/bin/bash

echo "🚀 SPÚŠŤAM CMEP PLATFORM"
echo "========================================="

# 1. Zabi staré procesy
echo ""
echo "📋 Zastavujem staré procesy..."
pkill -f uvicorn 2>/dev/null
pkill -f "python.*main" 2>/dev/null
sleep 2

# 2. Nastav prostredie
echo ""
echo "📋 Nastavujem prostredie..."
cd ~/waste-platform
export PYTHONPATH="${PYTHONPATH}:${PWD}"

# 3. Aktivuj virtuálne prostredie (ak existuje)
if [ -f "venv/bin/activate" ]; then
    source venv/bin/activate
fi

# 4. Spusti aplikáciu
echo ""
echo "📋 Spúšťam aplikáciu na porte 8000..."
cd app
uvicorn main:app --reload --port 8000 --host 0.0.0.0

  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"nonexistent@example.com","password":"wrongpass"}' || true

nohup python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8011 > "$HOME/cmep-backend-8011.log" 2>&1 &
sleep 4
tail -n 120 "$HOME/cmep-backend-8011.log" || true

curl -i -m 10 http://localhost:8011/health || true
echo

  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"nonexistent@example.com","password":"wrongpass"}' || true

python - <<'PY'
import socket
s = socket.socket()
s.settimeout(3)
try:
    s.connect(("127.0.0.1", 5432))
    print("TCP_CONNECT=OK")
except Exception as e:
    print("TCP_CONNECT=FAIL", repr(e))
finally:
    s.close()
PY
echo

echo

echo '===== PSQL DATABASE LIST ====='
PGPASSWORD=recyclehub psql -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 -U recyclehub -d recyclehub -c 'l' || true

--- ./classify_git_env_secrets.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

echo "============================================================"
echo " WASTE PLATFORM - SECRET VALUE CLASSIFICATION"
echo "============================================================"

echo
echo "=== REPO ==="
git rev-parse --show-toplevel

echo
echo "=== ENV FILE CLASSIFICATION ==="

git ls-files -z |
while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do

    case "$f" in
        .env|.env.*|*/.env|*/.env.*)

            echo
            echo "----- $f -----"

            awk '
            /^[[:space:]]*#/ { next }
            /^[[:space:]]*$/ { next }

            /^[[:space:]]*[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*[[:space:]]*=/ {

                eq = index($0, "=")

                key = substr($0, 1, eq - 1)
                value = substr($0, eq + 1)

                gsub(/[[:space:]]/, "", key)
                gsub(/^[[:space:]]+/, "", value)
                gsub(/[[:space:]]+$/, "", value)

                classification = "POTENTIAL REAL VALUE"

                if (value == "") {
                    classification = "PLACEHOLDER/EMPTY"
                }

                if (value ~ /^<.*>$/) {
                    classification = "PLACEHOLDER/REDACTED"
                }

                if (value ~ /^\$\{.*\}$/) {
                    classification = "PLACEHOLDER/TEMPLATE"
                }

                if (value == "CHANGE_ME" ||
                    value == "CHANGEME" ||
                    value == "REPLACE_ME" ||
                    value == "YOUR_VALUE" ||
                    value == "YOUR_SECRET" ||
                    value == "placeholder" ||
                    value == "PLACEHOLDER" ||
                    value == "TODO" ||
                    value == "FIXME" ||
                    value == "xxx" ||
                    value == "XXX") {
                    classification = "PLACEHOLDER"
                }

                if (value == "true" ||
                    value == "false" ||
                    value == "null" ||
                    value == "none" ||
                    value == "localhost" ||
                    value == "127.0.0.1" ||
                    value ~ /^[0-9]+$/ ||
                    value ~ /^https?:\/\/localhost/) {
                    classification = "NON-SECRET-LIKE"
                }

                print key " => " classification
            }
            ' "$f"

            ;;
    esac

done

echo
echo "=== SECRET-LIKE TRACKED FILES ==="

git ls-files |
grep -Ei '(^|/)(\.env|.*secret.*|.*credential.*|.*password.*|.*token.*|.*private.*)' |
sort -u

echo
echo "=== UNREACHABLE COMMIT ==="

git show --no-patch \
    --format='%H%n%ad%n%an%n%s' \
    15803461fbebf22bea8dbd3e7dd320a13f5d40bb \
    2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " CLASSIFICATION HOTOVÁ"
echo "============================================================"

--- ./create-recyclehub-db.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -eu

export PGPASSWORD='recyclehub'

psql \
  -h 127.0.0.1 \
  -p 5432 \
  -U recyclehub \
  -d postgres \
  -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 \
  -c "CREATE DATABASE recyclehub OWNER recyclehub;" 2>&1 || {
    echo "Database may already exist; continuing"
}

pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432
psql \
  -h 127.0.0.1 \
  -p 5432 \
  -U recyclehub \
  -d recyclehub \
  -c 'select current_database(), current_user;'

--- ./crypto_abi_diagnose.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

PYTHON="$(command -v python)"
PYLIB="/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/lib/libpython3.14.so"
RUST="/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/lib/python3.14/site-packages/cryptography/hazmat/bindings/_rust.abi3.so"

echo "============================================================"
echo " WASTE PLATFORM - CRYPTO ABI FORENSIC"
echo "============================================================"

echo
echo "[1] SYSTEM"
echo "Python:"
echo "$PYTHON"

echo
echo "Python version:"
python --version

echo
echo "Architecture:"
uname -m

echo
echo "Android:"
getprop ro.build.version.release 2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "ABI:"
getprop ro.product.cpu.abi 2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "Secondary ABI:"
getprop ro.product.cpu.abilist 2>/dev/null || true

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [2] PYTHON CONFIG"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import sys
import sysconfig
import ctypes

print("sys.executable:")
print(sys.executable)

print()
print("sys.prefix:")
print(sys.prefix)

print()
print("Python version:")
print(sys.version)

print()
print("SOABI:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("SOABI"))

print()
print("EXT_SUFFIX:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("EXT_SUFFIX"))

print()
print("LDLIBRARY:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("LDLIBRARY"))

print()
print("LIBDIR:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("LIBDIR"))

print()
print("MULTIARCH:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("MULTIARCH"))

print()
print("Py_DEBUG:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("Py_DEBUG"))

print()
print("WITH_PYMALLOC:")
print(sysconfig.get_config_var("WITH_PYMALLOC"))

print()
print("ctypes python:")
try:
    py = ctypes.PyDLL(None)
    addr = ctypes.cast(
        getattr(py, "PyLong_Type"),
        ctypes.c_void_p
    ).value

    print("PyLong_Type address:", hex(addr))

except Exception as e:
    print("PyLong_Type lookup through process: FAIL")
    print(repr(e))
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [3] FILES"
echo "============================================================"

echo
echo "libpython:"
ls -lh "$PYLIB" 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "_rust.abi3.so:"
ls -lh "$RUST" 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "file:"
file "$PYLIB" 2>&1 || true
file "$RUST" 2>&1 || true

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [4] LIBPYTHON SYMBOL"
echo "============================================================"

if command -v nm >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo
    echo "Searching PyLong_Type in libpython..."

    nm -D "$PYLIB" 2>/dev/null \
        | grep -E 'PyLong_Type|PyType_Type' \
        || echo "NO SYMBOL FOUND"

else
    echo "nm is not installed"
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [5] RUST UNDEFINED SYMBOLS"
echo "============================================================"

if command -v readelf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    readelf -Ws "$RUST" 2>/dev/null \
        | grep -E 'PyLong_Type|UND' \
        | grep -E 'PyLong_Type|UND' \
        | head -100 \
        || true
else
    echo "readelf is not installed"
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [6] RUST NEEDED LIBRARIES"
echo "============================================================"

if command -v readelf >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    readelf -d "$RUST" 2>/dev/null \
        | grep -E 'NEEDED|SONAME|RPATH|RUNPATH' \
        || true
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [7] LD LIBRARY"
echo "============================================================"

echo "LD_LIBRARY_PATH:"
echo "${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-<empty>}"

echo
echo "LD_PRELOAD:"
echo "${LD_PRELOAD:-<empty>}"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [8] CRYPTOGRAPHY"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import cryptography
print("cryptography:", cryptography.__version__)
print("location:", cryptography.__file__)

try:
    import cryptography.hazmat.bindings._rust as rust
    print("_rust import: OK")
    print("_rust location:", rust.__file__)
except Exception as e:
    print("_rust import: FAIL")
    print(repr(e))
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " [9] DIRECT DLOPEN"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import ctypes

PYLIB="/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/lib/libpython3.14.so"
RUST="/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/lib/python3.14/site-packages/cryptography/hazmat/bindings/_rust.abi3.so"

print("Loading libpython with RTLD_GLOBAL...")

try:
    py = ctypes.CDLL(PYLIB, mode=ctypes.RTLD_GLOBAL)
    print("libpython GLOBAL: OK")
except Exception as e:
    print("libpython GLOBAL: FAIL")
    print(repr(e))
    raise SystemExit(1)

print()

print("Loading Rust extension...")

try:
    rust = ctypes.CDLL(RUST, mode=ctypes.RTLD_GLOBAL)
    print("_rust: OK")
except Exception as e:
    print("_rust: FAIL")
    print(repr(e))
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " DIAGNOSTIKA HOTOVA"
echo "============================================================"

--- ./crypto_deep_diag.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

set -u

PY="$(command -v python)"
PYVER="$("$PY" -c 'import sys; print(f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}")')"
LIBPY="$PREFIX/lib/libpython${PYVER}.so"
RUST="$PREFIX/lib/python${PYVER}/site-packages/cryptography/hazmat/bindings/_rust.abi3.so"

echo "============================================================"
echo " WASTE PLATFORM - CRYPTO DEEP DIAGNOSTIC"
echo "============================================================"

echo
echo "[1] SYSTEM"
uname -a
echo "Android:"
getprop ro.build.version.release 2>/dev/null || true
echo "ABI:"
getprop ro.product.cpu.abi 2>/dev/null || true
echo "PREFIX:"
echo "$PREFIX"

echo
echo "[2] PYTHON"
"$PY" --version
echo "Executable:"
echo "$PY"
echo "SOABI:"
"$PY" -c 'import sysconfig; print(sysconfig.get_config_var("SOABI"))'
echo "EXT_SUFFIX:"
"$PY" -c 'import sysconfig; print(sysconfig.get_config_var("EXT_SUFFIX"))'

echo
echo "[3] LIBPYTHON"
echo "$LIBPY"

ls -lh "$LIBPY"

echo
echo "PyLong_Type:"
nm -D "$LIBPY" 2>/dev/null | grep ' PyLong_Type$' || true

echo
echo "PyType_Type:"
nm -D "$LIBPY" 2>/dev/null | grep ' PyType_Type$' || true

echo
echo "[4] RUST EXTENSION"
echo "$RUST"

ls -lh "$RUST"

echo
echo "ELF:"
file "$RUST"

echo
echo "[5] PYTHON SYMBOLS REQUIRED BY RUST"

readelf -Ws "$RUST" 2>/dev/null |
awk '$7 == "UND" && $8 ~ /^Py/ {print $8}' |
sort -u |
head -100

echo
echo "[6] PYTHON SYMBOLS PROVIDED BY LIBPYTHON"

for sym in \
    PyLong_Type \
    PyType_Type \
    PyModule_Type \
    PyUnicode_Type \
    PyBytes_Type \
    PyExc_TypeError \
    PyExc_ValueError \
    PyObject_CallNoArgs \
    PyObject_Vectorcall \
    PyImport_Import
do
    printf "%-35s " "$sym"

    if nm -D "$LIBPY" 2>/dev/null |
       grep -q " $sym$"; then
        echo "PROVIDED"
    else
        echo "MISSING"
    fi
done

echo
echo "[7] PYTHON BUILD CONFIG"

"$PY" - <<'PY'
import sys
import sysconfig

print("version:", sys.version)
print("implementation:", sys.implementation)
print("SOABI:", sysconfig.get_config_var("SOABI"))
print("MULTIARCH:", sysconfig.get_config_var("MULTIARCH"))
print("LDLIBRARY:", sysconfig.get_config_var("LDLIBRARY"))
print("LIBDIR:", sysconfig.get_config_var("LIBDIR"))
print("Py_ENABLE_SHARED:", sysconfig.get_config_var("Py_ENABLE_SHARED"))
print("CONFIG_ARGS:", sysconfig.get_config_var("CONFIG_ARGS"))
PY

echo
echo "[8] CRYPTOGRAPHY METADATA"

"$PY" -m pip show cryptography pyOpenSSL python-jose 2>/dev/null |
grep -E '^(Name|Version|Location):' || true

echo
echo "[9] OPENSSL"

openssl version -a 2>/dev/null | head -20 || true

echo
echo "[10] CRYPTOGRAPHY RUST DEPENDENCIES"

readelf -d "$RUST" 2>/dev/null |
grep -E 'NEEDED|RUNPATH|RPATH' || true

echo
echo "[11] RUST RELOCATION / SYMBOL TABLE"

readelf -Ws "$RUST" 2>/dev/null |
grep -E 'PyLong_Type|PyType_Type|PyObject|PyExc_' |
head -80

echo
echo "[12] DIRECT IMPORT"

"$PY" - <<'PY'
import sys
print("Python:", sys.version)

try:
    import cryptography
    print("cryptography:", cryptography.__version__)
except Exception as e:
    print("cryptography import FAIL:", repr(e))

try:
    from cryptography.hazmat.bindings._rust import exceptions
    print("_rust: OK")
except Exception as e:
    print("_rust: FAIL")
    print(type(e).__name__ + ":", str(e))
    print("repr:", repr(e))

try:
    import ctypes

    lib = ctypes.CDLL(
        sys.prefix + "/lib/libpython"
        + f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}.so",
        mode=ctypes.RTLD_GLOBAL
    )

    print("libpython RTLD_GLOBAL: OK")

    addr = ctypes.cast(
        getattr(lib, "PyLong_Type"),
        ctypes.c_void_p
    ).value

    print("PyLong_Type runtime address:", hex(addr))

except Exception as e:
    print("ctypes PyLong_Type test FAIL:", repr(e))

print()
print("DIAGNOSTIKA DOKONČENÁ")
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " END"
echo "============================================================"

--- ./diagnose_waste_platform.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

set +e

PROJECT="$HOME/waste-platform"
cd "$PROJECT" || exit 1

OUT="waste_platform_diagnosis_$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S).txt"

exec > >(tee "$OUT") 2>&1

echo "============================================================"
echo " WASTE PLATFORM - DEEP DIAGNOSTIC"
echo "============================================================"
echo "Project: $PROJECT"
echo "Date:    $(date)"
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo "1. PYTHON"
echo "============================================================"

python --version
python -m pip --version
which python
which pip

echo
python - <<'PY'
import sys
print("Python executable:", sys.executable)
print("Python version:", sys.version)
print("sys.path:")
for p in sys.path:
    print(" ", p)
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "2. PROJECT STRUCTURE"
echo "============================================================"

find . -maxdepth 2 -type d \
  -not -path './.git*' \
  -not -path './app.backup*' \
  -not -path './.fix-backup*' \
  -not -path './.schema-authority-backup*' \
  | sort

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "3. DEPENDENCY FILES"
echo "============================================================"

for f in \
    requirements.txt \
    app/requirements.txt \
    pyproject.toml \
    setup.py \
    setup.cfg \
    pytest.ini \
    alembic.ini
do
    if [ -f "$f" ]; then
        echo
        echo "----- $f -----"
        sed -n '1,260p' "$f"
    fi
done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "4. ACTIVE PYTHON FILES"
echo "============================================================"

find app \
  -type f \
  -name '*.py' \
  -not -path '*/__pycache__/*' \
  | sort

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "5. ACTIVE IMPORTS - app ONLY"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import ast
import pathlib
from collections import Counter

root = pathlib.Path("app")
imports = Counter()

for path in root.rglob("*.py"):
    if "__pycache__" in path.parts:
        continue

    try:
        source = path.read_text(errors="ignore")
        tree = ast.parse(source, filename=str(path))
    except Exception as e:
        print("PARSE ERROR:", path, e)
        continue

    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            for n in node.names:
                imports[n.name.split(".")[0]] += 1

        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            if node.module:
                imports[node.module.split(".")[0]] += 1

print("IMPORT                         COUNT")
print("-" * 45)

for name, count in imports.most_common():
    print(f"{name:30} {count}")
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "6. PYTHON SYNTAX CHECK"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import ast
import pathlib

root = pathlib.Path("app")
errors = 0
files = 0

for path in root.rglob("*.py"):
    if "__pycache__" in path.parts:
        continue

    files += 1

    try:
        ast.parse(
            path.read_text(errors="ignore"),
            filename=str(path)
        )
    except Exception as e:
        errors += 1
        print("[SYNTAX ERROR]", path)
        print(" ", e)

print()
print("Python files:", files)
print("Syntax errors:", errors)
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "7. APP IMPORT"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import importlib

mods = [
    "app",
    "app.main",
]

for mod in mods:
    try:
        m = importlib.import_module(mod)
        print("[OK]  ", mod, getattr(m, "__file__", ""))
    except Exception as e:
        print("[FAIL]", mod)
        print(type(e).__name__ + ":", e)
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "8. MAIN / FASTAPI ENTRYPOINTS"
echo "============================================================"

find app -type f -name '*.py' \
  -not -path '*/__pycache__/*' \
  -print0 |
while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
    if grep -qE \
      'FastAPI|uvicorn|create_app|app *=' \
      "$f" 2>/dev/null; then

        echo
        echo "----- $f -----"
        grep -nE \
          'FastAPI|uvicorn|create_app|app *=|include_router' \
          "$f" 2>/dev/null | head -100
    fi
done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "9. DATABASE CONFIGURATION"
echo "============================================================"

grep -RniE \
  'DATABASE_URL|DATABASE_URI|postgres|postgresql|sqlite|asyncpg|psycopg|create_engine|create_async_engine' \
  app \
  --include='*.py' \
  --exclude-dir='__pycache__' \
  2>/dev/null \
  | head -300

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "10. ALEMBIC"
echo "============================================================"

if command -v alembic >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    alembic current 2>&1
    echo
    alembic heads 2>&1
    echo
    alembic history --verbose 2>&1 | head -200
else
    echo "alembic command not found"
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "11. PYTEST DISCOVERY"
echo "============================================================"

if command -v pytest >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    pytest --collect-only -q 2>&1 | tail -100
else
    echo "pytest command not found"
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "12. PIP CHECK"
echo "============================================================"

python -m pip check

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "13. CRITICAL PACKAGE VERSIONS"
echo "============================================================"

python - <<'PY'
import importlib.metadata as md

packages = [
    "fastapi",
    "starlette",
    "pydantic",
    "pydantic-settings",
    "sqlalchemy",
    "alembic",

for name in packages:
    try:
        print(f"{name:25} {md.version(name)}")
    except md.PackageNotFoundError:
        print(f"{name:25} NOT INSTALLED")
PY

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo "14. GIT STATUS"
echo "============================================================"

git status --short

echo

--- ./fix_auth_complete.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
# KOMPLETNÁ OPRAVA AUTHENTIKÁCIE PRE WASTE-PLATFORM

set -e

echo "=========================================="
echo "OPRAVA AUTHENTIKÁCIE – WASTE-PLATFORM"
echo "=========================================="

# 1. PRIDANIE CHÝBAJÚCEHO STĹPCA do DB
echo ""
echo "[1] Pridanie stĺpca hashed_password do tabuľky users..."

psql -d cmep << 'SQL'
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
        WHERE table_name='users' AND column_name='hashed_password'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN hashed_password VARCHAR(255);
        RAISE NOTICE 'Stĺpec hashed_password bol pridaný.';
    ELSE
        RAISE NOTICE 'Stĺpec hashed_password už existuje.';
    END IF;
END $$;
SQL

# 2. SKOPÍROVANIE ÚDAJOV z password_hash do hashed_password
echo ""
echo "[2] Kopírovanie hesiel do hashed_password..."

psql -d cmep -c "UPDATE users SET hashed_password = password_hash WHERE hashed_password IS NULL;"

# 3. KONTROLA, ČI EXISTUJE get_async_session v session.py
echo ""
echo "[3] Kontrola get_async_session v session.py..."

if ! grep -q "get_async_session" app/shared/infrastructure/db/session.py; then
    echo 'get_async_session = get_db_session' >> app/shared/infrastructure/db/session.py
    echo "✅ get_async_session pridané do session.py"
else
    echo "✅ get_async_session už existuje"
fi

# 4. VYTVORENIE TESTOVACIEHO POUŽÍVATEĽA (ak neexistuje)
echo ""
echo "[4] Vytvorenie testovacieho používateľa..."

HASH=$(python3 -c "import bcrypt; print(bcrypt.hashpw(b'test123', bcrypt.gensalt()).decode())")

psql -d cmep << SQL
INSERT INTO users (id, email, password_hash, hashed_password, first_name, last_name, status, created_at, updated_at)
SELECT 
    gen_random_uuid()::text,
    'test@example.com',
    '$HASH',
    '$HASH',
    'Test',
    'User',
    'active',
    NOW(),
    NOW()
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = 'test@example.com'
);
SQL

echo "✅ Testovací používateľ: test@example.com / test123"

# 5. AKTIVÁCIA VŠETKÝCH POUŽÍVATEĽOV
echo ""
echo "[5] Aktivácia všetkých používateľov..."

psql -d cmep -c "UPDATE users SET status = 'active' WHERE status IS NULL OR status != 'active';"

# 6. REŠTARTOVANIE BACKENDU
echo ""
echo "[6] Reštart backendu..."

pkill -f "uvicorn" 2>/dev/null || true
sleep 2

cd ~/waste-platform
nohup python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8001 >> ~/cmep-backend.log 2>&1 &
sleep 3

# 7. TESTOVANIE PRIHLÁSENIA
echo ""
echo "[7] Testovanie prihlásenia..."

curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8001/api/v1/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"email":"test@example.com","password":"test123"}' | python3 -c "
import sys, json
try:
    data = json.load(sys.stdin)
    if 'access_token' in data:
        print('✅ PRIHLÁSENIE ÚSPEŠNÉ!')
        print('Token:', data.get('access_token', '')[:50] + '...')
    elif 'detail' in data:
        print('❌ Chyba:', data.get('detail'))
    else:
        print('❌ Neznáma odpoveď:', data)
except:
    print('❌ Chyba pri parsovaní odpovede')
"

echo ""
echo "=========================================="
echo "   HOTOVO!"
echo "=========================================="
echo ""
echo "Prihlasovacie údaje:"
echo "  Email: test@example.com"
echo "  Heslo: test123"
echo ""
echo "Swagger UI: http://localhost:8001/docs"
echo "Frontend:   http://localhost:3001"
echo ""

--- ./init-postgres-termux.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -eu

PREFIX="${PREFIX:-/data/data/com.termux/files/usr}"
PGDATA="${PREFIX}/var/lib/postgresql"
PGLOG="${HOME}/postgresql-waste-platform.log"

if [ -f "$PGDATA/PG_VERSION" ]; then
    echo "Cluster already exists: $PGDATA"
    exit 0
fi

mkdir -p "$PGDATA"

initdb \
  -D "$PGDATA" \
  --auth-local=trust \
  --auth-host=scram-sha-256 \
  --username=recyclehub \
  --pwfile=<(printf '%s
' 'recyclehub')

cat >> "$PGDATA/postgresql.conf" <<'CONF'
listen_addresses = '127.0.0.1'
port = 5432
CONF

pg_ctl \
  -D "$PGDATA" \
  -l "$PGLOG" \
  -o "-h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432" \
  start \
  -w

pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432

--- ./move_audit_to_phone.sh ---
#!/bin/bash

echo "📦 PRESÚVAM AUDIT VÝSLEDKY DO TELEFÓNU"
echo "========================================="

# 1. Zisti posledný audit
AUDIT_FILE=$(ls -t FINAL-AUTHORITY-AUDIT-*.txt 2>/dev/null | head -1)
if [ -z "$AUDIT_FILE" ]; then
    echo "❌ Žiadny auditný súbor nenájdený!"
    exit 1
fi

echo "📋 Auditný súbor: $AUDIT_FILE"

# 2. Skopíruj audit do Download
echo ""
echo "📋 Kopírujem audit do /sdcard/Download/..."
cp "$AUDIT_FILE" /sdcard/Download/
echo "✅ Audit skopírovaný"

# 3. Skopíruj aj ďalšie dôležité súbory
echo ""
echo "📋 Kopírujem ďalšie dôležité súbory..."
cp STEP-09-SCHEMA-PROVENANCE-AUDIT-*.txt /sdcard/Download/ 2>/dev/null || echo "ℹ️  Žiadny STEP-09 súbor"
cp .env /sdcard/Download/waste-platform.env 2>/dev/null || echo "ℹ️  Žiadny .env súbor"
cp alembic.ini /sdcard/Download/waste-platform-alembic.ini 2>/dev/null || echo "ℹ️  Žiadny alembic.ini"

# 4. Vytvor archív
echo ""
echo "📋 Vytváram archív..."
ARCHIVE_NAME="waste-platform-audit-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S).tar.gz"
tar -czf "/sdcard/Download/$ARCHIVE_NAME" \
    FINAL-AUTHORITY-AUDIT-*.txt \
    STEP-09-SCHEMA-PROVENANCE-AUDIT-*.txt \
    .env \
    alembic.ini \
    2>/dev/null

echo "✅ Archív vytvorený: /sdcard/Download/$ARCHIVE_NAME"

# 5. Zobraz výsledok
echo ""
echo "========================================="
echo "📊 VÝSLEDOK PRESUNU"
echo "========================================="
echo ""
echo "📁 Súbory v /sdcard/Download/:"
ls -lh /sdcard/Download/FINAL-AUTHORITY-AUDIT-*.txt 2>/dev/null
echo ""
ls -lh /sdcard/Download/waste-platform-audit-*.tar.gz 2>/dev/null
echo ""
echo "📊 VEĽKOSŤ ARCHÍVU:"
du -sh /sdcard/Download/waste-platform-audit-*.tar.gz 2>/dev/null
echo ""
echo "========================================="
echo "✅ HOTOVO!"
echo "========================================="

--- ./rebuild-sqlite-initial-migration.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -eu

PROJECT="$HOME/waste-platform"
cd "$PROJECT"

echo "===== 1. LIST MIGRATION FILES ====="
find migrations -maxdepth 3 -type f | sort

echo
echo "===== 2. BACKUP CURRENT DB + MIGRATIONS ====="
STAMP="$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)"
mkdir -p "$HOME/waste-platform-backups"

[ -f var/cmep.db ] && cp -av var/cmep.db "$HOME/waste-platform-backups/cmep.db.$STAMP.bak" || true
[ -d migrations/versions ] && cp -av migrations/versions "$HOME/waste-platform-backups/versions.$STAMP.bak" || true

echo
echo "===== 3. REMOVE OLD SQLITE FILE ====="
rm -f var/cmep.db
mkdir -p var

echo
echo "===== 4. SHOW OLD VERSION FILE CONTENT ====="
if [ -f migrations/versions/001_initial.py ]; then
  sed -n '1,220p' migrations/versions/001_initial.py
else
  echo "001_initial.py NOT FOUND"
fi

echo
echo "===== 5. REMOVE OLD VERSION FILES ====="
find migrations/versions -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.py' ! -name '__init__.py' -print -delete || true
find migrations/versions -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.pyc' -print -delete || true

echo
echo "===== 6. VERIFY MODEL IMPORT + TABLE COUNT ====="
python - <<'PY'
import app.shared.infrastructure.db.register_models  # noqa: F401
from app.shared.infrastructure.db.base import Base

tables = sorted(Base.metadata.tables.keys())
print("TABLE_COUNT =", len(tables))
for name in tables:
    print(name)
PY

echo
echo "===== 7. AUTOGENERATE INITIAL MIGRATION ====="
alembic revision --autogenerate -m "initial_schema"

echo
echo "===== 8. SHOW GENERATED MIGRATION ====="
LATEST="$(find migrations/versions -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.py' ! -name '__init__.py' | sort | tail -n 1)"
echo "LATEST=$LATEST"
sed -n '1,260p' "$LATEST"

echo
echo "===== 9. APPLY MIGRATION ====="
alembic upgrade head

echo
echo "===== 10. SHOW ALEMBIC STATE ====="
alembic heads || true
alembic current || true

echo
echo "===== 11. SQLITE TABLE INVENTORY ====="
python - <<'PY'
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

db = Path("var/cmep.db")
print("DB_PATH =", db.resolve())
print("DB_EXISTS =", db.exists())

if db.exists():
    con = sqlite3.connect(db)
    rows = con.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name").fetchall()
    for row in rows:
        print(row[0])
    con.close()
PY

echo
echo "===== 12. CHECK EXPECTED TABLES ====="
python - <<'PY'
import sqlite3

expected = {
    "admin_audit_entries",
    "analytics_events",
    "companies",
    "company_memberships",
    "compliance_records",
    "documents",
    "inquiries",
    "listings",
    "notifications",
    "refresh_sessions",
    "users",
    "alembic_version",
}

con = sqlite3.connect("var/cmep.db")
actual = {r[0] for r in con.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
con.close()

print("MISSING =", sorted(expected - actual))
print("EXTRA =", sorted(actual - expected))
print("ALL_OK =", expected.issubset(actual))
PY

echo
echo "===== 13. ALEMBIC CHECK ====="
alembic check || true

--- ./remove-empty-alembic-revision.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -eu

PROJECT="$HOME/waste-platform"
EMPTY_REVISION="eb259b8c673a"
EMPTY_FILE="$PROJECT/migrations/versions/${EMPTY_REVISION}_popis_zmeny.py"

cd "$PROJECT"

echo "===== BEFORE ====="
alembic current
alembic heads
alembic history --verbose | head -n 80

if [ ! -f "$EMPTY_FILE" ]; then
  echo "ERROR: Expected empty migration was not found:"
  echo "$EMPTY_FILE"
  exit 1
fi

echo
echo "===== VERIFY EMPTY MIGRATION ====="
sed -n '1,220p' "$EMPTY_FILE"

if grep -Eq 'op\.(create_table|drop_table|add_column|drop_column|alter_column|create_index|drop_index|create_foreign_key|drop_constraint)' "$EMPTY_FILE"; then
  echo "ERROR: Migration contains schema operations; refusing deletion."
  exit 1
fi

echo
echo "===== DOWNGRADE ONLY EMPTY REVISION ====="
alembic downgrade -1

echo
echo "===== REMOVE EMPTY REVISION FILE ====="
mkdir -p "$HOME/waste-platform-backups"
cp -av "$EMPTY_FILE" "$HOME/waste-platform-backups/$(basename "$EMPTY_FILE").$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).bak"
rm -f "$EMPTY_FILE"

find migrations -type d -name '__pycache__' -prune -exec rm -rf {} +

echo
echo "===== AFTER ====="
alembic heads
alembic current
alembic check

echo
echo "===== VERSION FILES ====="
find migrations/versions -maxdepth 1 -type f -name '*.py' -print | sort

--- ./reset-initial-migration-sqlite.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -eu

mkdir -p "$HOME/waste-platform-backups"
[ -f var/cmep.db ] && cp -av var/cmep.db "$HOME/waste-platform-backups/cmep.db.$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).bak" || true
[ -f migrations/versions/001_initial.py ] && cp -av migrations/versions/001_initial.py "$HOME/waste-platform-backups/001_initial.py.$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).bak" || true

rm -f migrations/versions/001_initial.py
rm -f var/cmep.db

alembic revision --autogenerate -m "initial_schema"
alembic upgrade head
alembic current
alembic check || true

python - <<'PY'
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

expected = {
    "admin_audit_entries",
    "analytics_events",
    "companies",
    "company_memberships",
    "compliance_records",
    "documents",
    "inquiries",
    "listings",
    "notifications",
    "refresh_sessions",
    "users",
    "alembic_version",
}

db = Path("var/cmep.db")
print("DB_EXISTS =", db.exists())
if db.exists():
    con = sqlite3.connect(db)
    actual = {r[0] for r in con.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
    con.close()
    print("MISSING =", sorted(expected - actual))
    print("EXTRA =", sorted(actual - expected))
PY

--- ./run-check.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -e
echo "PWD: $(pwd)"
echo
echo "== config files =="
find . -maxdepth 3 ( -name "package.json" -o -name "docker-compose.yml" -o -name "compose.yaml" -o -name "requirements.txt" -o -name "pyproject.toml" -o -name "vite.config.*" -o -name "next.config.*" ) | sort
echo
echo "== package scripts =="
find . -maxdepth 3 -name package.json -exec sh -c '
  f="$1"
  echo
  echo "### $f"
  sed -n "1,220p" "$f"
' _ {} ;

--- ./scripts/backup.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
PROJECT_DIR="${PROJECT_DIR:-/opt/cmep-platform}"
ENV_FILE="${ENV_FILE:-.env.production}"
BACKUP_ROOT="${BACKUP_ROOT:-${PROJECT_DIR}/backups}"
TIMESTAMP="$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)"
BACKUP_DIR="${BACKUP_ROOT}/${TIMESTAMP}"
mkdir -p "${BACKUP_DIR}"
cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"
set -a
source "${ENV_FILE}"
set +a
echo "[backup] creating backup directory ${BACKUP_DIR}"
echo "[backup] backing up postgres"
docker compose --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" exec -T postgres pg_dump -U "${POSTGRES_USER}" -d "${POSTGRES_DB}" > "${BACKUP_DIR}/postgres.sql"
echo "[backup] done: ${BACKUP_DIR}"

--- ./scripts/bootstrap-letsencrypt.sh ---
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
PROJECT_DIR="${PROJECT_DIR:-/opt/cmep-platform}"
ENV_FILE="${ENV_FILE:-.env.production}"
cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"
set -a
source "${ENV_FILE}"
set +a
if [ -z "${APP_DOMAIN:-}" ] || [ -z "${API_DOMAIN:-}" ] || [ -z "${LETSENCRYPT_EMAIL:-}" ]; then
  echo "APP_DOMAIN, API_DOMAIN and LETSENCRYPT_EMAIL are required in ${ENV_FILE}"
exit 1
fi
docker compose --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" up -d nginx
docker compose --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" run --rm certbot certonly 
  --webroot -w /var/www/certbot -d "${APP_DOMAIN}" -d "${API_DOMAIN}" 
  --email "${LETSENCRYPT_EMAIL}" --agree-tos --no-eff-email
docker compose --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" restart nginx
echo "Let's Encrypt certificates issued and nginx restarted."

--- ./scripts/deploy.sh ---
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail

PROJECT_DIR="${PROJECT_DIR:-/data/data/com.termux/files/home/waste-platform}"
ENV_FILE="${ENV_FILE:-.env.production}"
COMPOSE_FILE="${COMPOSE_FILE:-docker-compose.yml}"

cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"

if [ ! -f "${ENV_FILE}" ]; then
    echo "[ERROR] Missing ${PROJECT_DIR}/${ENV_FILE}" >&2
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[ERROR] Docker is not installed or not available in PATH" >&2
    exit 1
fi

if ! docker compose version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[ERROR] Docker Compose plugin is not available" >&2
    exit 1
fi

set -a
. "./${ENV_FILE}"
set +a

if [ -f "infra/nginx/conf.d/default.conf.template" ]; then
    mkdir -p infra/nginx/conf.d

    if command -v envsubst >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        envsubst '${APP_DOMAIN} ${API_DOMAIN}' \
            < infra/nginx/conf.d/default.conf.template \
            > infra/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
    else
        echo "[WARN] envsubst is not installed; Nginx template was not rendered"
    fi
fi

docker compose \
    --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" \
    -f "${COMPOSE_FILE}" \
    config >/dev/null

docker compose \
    --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" \
    -f "${COMPOSE_FILE}" \
    up -d --build --remove-orphans

echo "[OK] Deployment completed"

--- ./scripts/forensic-audit.sh ---
#!/bin/bash

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#              FORENZNÝ AUDIT PLATFORMY – CMEP (Waste Platform)
#              Vykonané: $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

AUDIT_REPORT="forensic-audit-report-$(date '+%Y%m%d-%H%M%S').txt"
PROJECT_ROOT="$(pwd)"
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color
BOLD='\033[1m'

# Reset
echo "" > "$AUDIT_REPORT"

log() {
    echo -e "$1" | tee -a "$AUDIT_REPORT"
}

log_header() {
    log ""
    log "╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗"
    log "║  $1"
    log "╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝"
    log ""
}

log_section() {
    log ""
    log "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
    log "  📋 $1"
    log "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━"
}

count_pass=0
count_fail=0
count_warn=0
count_info=0

check_pass() {
    ((count_pass++))
    log "  ${GREEN}✅ PASS${NC}  $1"
}

check_fail() {
    ((count_fail++))
    log "  ${RED}❌ FAIL${NC}  $1"
}

check_warn() {
    ((count_warn++))
    log "  ${YELLOW}⚠️  WARN${NC}  $1"
}

check_info() {
    ((count_info++))
    log "  ${BLUE}ℹ️  INFO${NC}  $1"
}

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# ZAČIATOK AUDITU
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

log_header "${BOLD}FORENZNÝ AUDIT PLATFORMY CMEP${NC}"
log "Dátum: $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
log "Používateľ: $(whoami)"
log "Hostiteľ: $(hostname)"
log "Platforma: $(uname -s) $(uname -m)"
log "Adresár: $PROJECT_ROOT"
log ""
log "Verzia Docker: $(docker --version 2>/dev/null || echo 'Nedostupné')"
log "Verzia Docker Compose: $(docker compose version 2>/dev/null || docker-compose --version 2>/dev/null || echo 'Nedostupné')"

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SEKCIA 1: ANALÝZA ŠTRUKTÚRY PROJEKTU
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

log_header "1. ANALÝZA ŠTRUKTÚRY PROJEKTU"

log_section "1.1 Adresárová štruktúra"

if [ -d "$PROJECT_ROOT" ]; then
    check_pass "Koreňový adresár existuje"
    log "  Adresárová hierarchia:"
    find . -maxdepth 3 -type d ! -path '*/\.*' | head -50 | while read dir; do
        depth=$(echo "$dir" | tr -cd '/' | wc -c)
        indent=$(printf '%*s' $((depth * 2)) "")
        count=$(find "$dir" -maxdepth 1 -type f 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        log "  ${indent}📁 ${dir#$PROJECT_ROOT/} (${count} súborov)"
    done
else
    check_fail "Koreňový adresár neexistuje"
fi

log ""
log_section "1.2 Kľúčové súbory"

key_files=(
    "docker-compose.yml"
    "docker-compose.monitoring.yml"
    "Makefile"
    "README.md"
    ".env"
    ".env.production"
    "requirements.txt"
    "package.json"
    "pyproject.toml"
)

for file in "${key_files[@]}"; do
    if [ -f "$file" ]; then
        size=$(stat -f%z "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c%s "$file" 2>/dev/null)
        lines=$(wc -l < "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
        check_pass "Súbor existuje: $file (${size}B, ${lines} riadkov)"
    else
        check_warn "Súbor chýba: $file"
    fi
done

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SEKCIA 2: SYNTAX KONTROLA
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

log_header "2. SYNTAX KONTROLA"

log_section "2.1 Docker Compose súbory"

if command -v docker &> /dev/null; then
    if docker compose config > /dev/null 2>&1; then
        check_pass "docker-compose.yml je validný"
    else
        check_fail "docker-compose.yml má syntaktické chyby"
        docker compose config 2>&1 | head -20 | while read line; do
            log "     $line"
        done
    fi

    if [ -f "docker-compose.monitoring.yml" ]; then
        if docker compose -f docker-compose.monitoring.yml config > /dev/null 2>&1; then
            check_pass "docker-compose.monitoring.yml je validný"
        else
            check_fail "docker-compose.monitoring.yml má syntaktické chyby"
        fi
    fi
else
    check_warn "Docker nie je nainštalovaný - kontrola preskočená"
fi

log ""
log_section "2.2 YAML konfiguračné súbory"

yaml_files=$(find . -name "*.yml" -o -name "*.yaml" 2>/dev/null | grep -v node_modules | grep -v __pycache__)
if [ -z "$yaml_files" ]; then
    check_warn "Žiadne YAML súbory nenájdené"
else
    for yaml in $yaml_files; do
        if command -v python3 &> /dev/null; then
            if python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('$yaml'))" 2>/dev/null; then
                check_pass "YAML validný: $yaml"
            else
                check_fail "YAML nevalidný: $yaml"
            fi
        else
            # Ručná kontrola formátu
            if grep -q "^[[:space:]]*[^#:]*:.*[^:]$" "$yaml" 2>/dev/null; then
                check_info "YAML: $yaml (bez Python validátora)"
            fi
        fi
    done
fi

log ""
log_section "2.3 Python syntax kontrola"

py_files=$(find . -name "*.py" ! -path "*/\.*" ! -path "*/__pycache__/*" 2>/dev/null | head -30)
if [ -z "$py_files" ]; then
    check_warn "Žiadne Python súbory nenájdené"
else
    for py in $py_files; do
        if command -v python3 &> /dev/null; then
            if python3 -m py_compile "$py" 2>/dev/null; then
                check_pass "Python validný: $py"
            else
                check_fail "Python chyba: $py"
                python3 -m py_compile "$py" 2>&1 | while read line; do
                    log "     $line"
                done
            fi
        else
            check_info "Python interpreter nie je dostupný"
            break
        fi
    done
fi

log ""
log_section "2.4 Shell script syntax kontrola"

sh_files=$(find . -name "*.sh" 2>/dev/null | head -20)
for sh in $sh_files; do
    if [ -f "$sh" ]; then
        if bash -n "$sh" 2>/dev/null; then
            check_pass "Shell script validný: $sh"
        else
            check_fail "Shell script má chybu: $sh"
            bash -n "$sh" 2>&1 | while read line; do
                log "     $line"
            done
        fi
    fi
done

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SEKCIA 3: BEZPEČNOSTNÝ AUDIT
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

log_header "3. BEZPEČNOSTNÝ AUDIT"

log_section "3.1 Sekrety a konfigurácia"

if [ -f ".env" ]; then
    sensitive_patterns=("PASSWORD" "SECRET" "KEY" "TOKEN" "API_KEY")
    for pattern in "${sensitive_patterns[@]}"; do
        if grep -r "$pattern" .env 2>/dev/null | grep -v "^#" | grep -v "change-me" | grep -qv "example"; then
            check_warn "Možný hardcoded secret: $pattern v .env"
        fi
    done
fi

# Kontrola production .env
if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    if grep -q "change-me" .env.production 2>/dev/null; then
        check_warn ".env.production obsahuje placeholder sekrety (change-me)"
    fi
    if grep -q "your-secret-key" .env.production 2>/dev/null; then
        check_fail ".env.production obsahuje nebezpečné default hodnoty"
    fi
fi

log ""
log_section "3.2 Docker bezpečnosť"

if [ -f "docker-compose.yml" ]; then
    if grep -q "privileged.*true" docker-compose.yml 2>/dev/null; then
        check_warn "Služba beží v privileged režime"
    fi
    
    if grep -q "network_mode.*host" docker-compose.yml 2>/dev/null; then
        check_warn "Služba používa host network mode"
    fi
    
    if grep -q ":4444" docker-compose.yml 2>/dev/null || grep -q ":4443" docker-compose.yml 2>/dev/null; then
        check_warn "Služba odhaľuje port 4444/4443 (metabase?)"
    fi
    
    # Kontrola root user v Dockerfile
    df_count=$(find . -name "Dockerfile*" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    if [ "$df_count" -gt 0 ]; then
        check_info "Nájdené $df_count Dockerfile súborov"
        find . -name "Dockerfile*" -exec grep -l "USER root" {} \; 2>/dev/null | while read df; do
            check_warn "Dockerfile môže beží ako root: $df"
        done
    fi
fi

log ""
log_section "3.3 CORS a autentifikácia"

if grep -q "CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS" .env.production 2>/dev/null; then
    cors_origins=$(grep "CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS" .env.production | cut -d'=' -f2)
    if [ "$cors_origins" = "*" ] || [ "$cors_origins" = "http://*:*" ]; then
        check_fail "CORS je otvorený pre všetky domény"
    else
        check_pass "CORS je nakonfigurovaný"
    fi
fi

if grep -q "JWT_SECRET_KEY" .env.production 2>/dev/null; then
    jwt_secret=$(grep "JWT_SECRET_KEY" .env.production | cut -d'=' -f2)
    if [ ${#jwt_secret} -lt 32 ]; then
        check_warn "JWT_SECRET_KEY je príliš krátky (min. 32 znakov)"
    else
        check_pass "JWT_SECRET_KEY má dostatočnú dĺžku"
    fi
fi

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SEKCIA 4: FUNKCIONALITA A LOGIKA
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

log_header "4. FUNKCIONALITA A LOGIKA"

log_section "4.1 FastAPI aplikácia"

if [ -d "app" ]; then
    check_pass "Adresár app/ existuje"
    
    # Kontrola main.py
    if [ -f "app/main.py" ]; then
        check_pass "app/main.py existuje"
        
        # Import kontrol
        if grep -q "FastAPI" app/main.py 2>/dev/null; then
            check_pass "FastAPI import nájdený"
        fi
        
        if grep -q "include_router" app/main.py 2>/dev/null; then
            routers=$(grep -c "include_router" app/main.py)
            check_info "Počet routerov: $routers"
        fi
        
        # Health check
        if grep -q "/health" app/main.py 2>/dev/null; then
            check_pass "Health endpoint definovaný"
        else
            check_warn "Health endpoint chýba v main.py"
        fi
        
        # Metrics
        if grep -q "/metrics" app/main.py 2>/dev/null; then
            check_pass "Metrics endpoint definovaný"
        else
            check_warn "Metrics endpoint chýba v main.py"
        fi
    else
        check_warn "app/main.py chýba"
    fi
    
    # Routers
    router_count=$(find app -name "*router*.py" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    if [ "$router_count" -gt 0 ]; then
        check_pass "Nájdené $router_count router súborov"
    fi
    
    # Schemas
    schema_count=$(find app -path "*schema*.py" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    if [ "$schema_count" -gt 0 ]; then
        check_pass "Nájdené $schema_count schema súborov"
    fi
    
else
    check_fail "Adresár app/ neexistuje"
fi

log ""
log_section "4.2 Next.js aplikácia"

if [ -d "apps" ]; then
    check_pass "Adresár apps/ existuje"
    
    if [ -f "apps/web/package.json" ]; then
        check_pass "apps/web/package.json existuje"
        
        if grep -q '"next"' apps/web/package.json 2>/dev/null; then
            check_pass "Next.js závislosť definovaná"
        fi
        
        if grep -q '"typescript"' apps/web/package.json 2>/dev/null; then
            check_pass "TypeScript závislosť definovaná"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Src štruktúra
    if [ -d "apps/web/src" ]; then
        check_pass "apps/web/src/ existuje"
        
        app_files=$(find apps/web/src -name "page.tsx" -o -name "page.ts" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        if [ "$app_files" -gt 0 ]; then
            check_pass "Nájdené $app_files page súborov"
        fi
        
        component_count=$(find apps/web/src -name "*.tsx" ! -path "*/components/*" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        components=$(find apps/web/src -path "*/components/*" -name "*.tsx" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        check_info "Komponenty: $component_count súborov, $components v components/"
    fi
else
    check_warn "Adresár apps/ neexistuje alebo je prázdny"
fi

log ""
log_section "4.3 Databáza a ORM"

if [ -f "app/database.py" ] || [ -f "app/core/database.py" ] || find app -name "*database*.py" 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then
    check_pass "Databázové súbory nájdené"
    
    if grep -q "asyncpg" app/**/*.py 2>/dev/null || grep -q "asyncpg" app/**/**/*.py 2>/dev/null; then
        check_pass "asyncpg (PostgreSQL async driver) použitý"
    fi
    
    if grep -q "sqlalchemy" app/**/*.py 2>/dev/null || grep -q "sqlalchemy" app/**/**/*.py 2>/dev/null; then
        check_pass "SQLAlchemy ORM nájdený"
    fi
else
    check_warn "Databázové súbory neručné"
fi

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SEKCIA 5: PREPOJENIA A INTEGRÁCIE
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

log_header "5. PREPOJENIA A INTEGRÁCIE"

log_section "5.1 Docker služby"

log "Kontrola docker-compose.yml služieb:"
services=$(grep -E "^\s+[a-z_]+:" docker-compose.yml 2>/dev/null | sed 's/:$//' | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//' | sort | uniq)
for svc in $services; do
    if [ -n "$svc" ]; then
        check_info "Služba: $svc"
    fi
done

log ""
log_section "5.2 Environment premenné"

required_env_vars=(
    "DATABASE_URL"
    "REDIS_URL"
    "JWT_SECRET_KEY"
    "APP_HOST"
    "APP_PORT"
)

for var in "${required_env_vars[@]}"; do
    if grep -q "$var" .env.production 2>/dev/null; then
        check_pass "Env premenná definovaná: $var"
    else
        check_warn "Env premenná chýba: $var"
    fi
done

log ""
log_section "5.3 MinIO / S3 konfigurácia"

minio_vars=("MINIO_ROOT_USER" "MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD" "S3_BUCKET")
for var in "${minio_vars[@]}"; do
    if grep -q "$var" .env.production 2>/dev/null; then
        check_pass "MinIO konfigurácia: $var"
    else
        check_warn "MinIO konfigurácia chýba: $var"
    fi
done

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# SEKCIA 6: MONITORING A OBSERVABILITY

--- ./scripts/health-check.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
PROJECT_DIR="${PROJECT_DIR:-/opt/cmep-platform}"
ENV_FILE="${ENV_FILE:-.env.production}"
cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"
echo "[health] docker compose ps"
docker compose --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" ps
echo "[health] api"
curl -fsSL "${API_HEALTH_URL:-https://api.example.com/health}" >/dev/null && echo "api ok" || echo "api failed"
echo "[health] frontend"
curl -fsSL "${APP_HEALTH_URL:-https://app.example.com}" >/dev/null && echo "frontend ok" || echo "frontend failed"

--- ./scripts/load-test.sh ---
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail

TEST_NAME="${1:-smoke-test.js}"
BASE_URL="${BASE_URL:-http://localhost:8000}"

case "${TEST_NAME}" in
  smoke|smoke-test.js) SCRIPT="smoke-test.js" ;;
  load|load-test.js) SCRIPT="load-test.js" ;;
  breakpoint|breakpoint-test.js) SCRIPT="breakpoint-test.js" ;;
  *) SCRIPT="${TEST_NAME}" ;;
esac

docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.load.yml run --rm \
  -e BASE_URL="${BASE_URL}" \
  k6 run "/scripts/${SCRIPT}"

--- ./scripts/logs.sh ---
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
SERVICE="${1:-}"
PATTERN="${2:-}"
TAIL_LINES="${TAIL_LINES:-200}"
if [ -z "${SERVICE}" ]; then
echo "Usage: $0 <service> [pattern]"
  exit 1
fi
if [ -n "${PATTERN}" ]; then
docker compose logs --tail="${TAIL_LINES}" "${SERVICE}" | grep -i --color=always "${PATTERN}" || true
else
  docker compose logs --tail="${TAIL_LINES}" "${SERVICE}"
fi

--- ./scripts/restore.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
if [ $# -lt 1 ]; then
  echo "Usage: $0 <backup-directory>"
  exit 1
fi
BACKUP_DIR="$1"
PROJECT_DIR="${PROJECT_DIR:-/opt/cmep-platform}"
ENV_FILE="${ENV_FILE:-.env.production}"
if [ ! -d "${BACKUP_DIR}" ]; then
  echo "Backup directory not found: ${BACKUP_DIR}"
  exit 1
fi
cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"
set -a
source "${ENV_FILE}"
set +a
echo "[restore] restoring postgres"
cat "${BACKUP_DIR}/postgres.sql" | docker compose --env-file "${ENV_FILE}" exec -T postgres psql -U "${POSTGRES_USER}" -d "${POSTGRES_DB}"
echo "[restore] done"

--- ./scripts/validate-project.sh ---
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail

echo "=== CMEP Project Validation Script ==="
echo "Date: $(date -Iseconds)"

PROJECT_DIR="${PROJECT_DIR:-$(pwd)}"
cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"

ERRORS=0
WARNINGS=0

log_error() {
  echo "[ERROR] $1" >&2
  ((ERRORS++)) || true
}

log_warn() {
  echo "[WARN] $1"
  ((WARNINGS++)) || true
}

log_ok() {
  echo "[OK] $1"
}

# -------------------------------------------------
# 1. Check basic structure
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 1. Checking basic project structure ==="

REQUIRED_DIRS=(
  "app"
  "apps/web"
  "docs"
  "infra"
  "scripts"
)

for d in "${REQUIRED_DIRS[@]}"; do
  if [ -d "${d}" ]; then
    log_ok "Directory exists: ${d}"
  else
    log_error "Missing directory: ${d}"
  fi
done

REQUIRED_FILES=(
  "README.md"
  "docker-compose.yml"
  ".env.production"
  "app/main.py"
)

for f in "${REQUIRED_FILES[@]}"; do
  if [ -f "${f}" ]; then
    log_ok "File exists: ${f}"
  else
    log_error "Missing file: ${f}"
  fi
done

# -------------------------------------------------
# 2. Check key module directories
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 2. Checking backend module structure ==="

MODULES=(
  "auth"
  "companies"
  "users"
  "company_memberships"
  "listings"
  "inquiries"
  "documents"
  "compliance"
  "notifications"
  "analytics"
  "admin"
)

for m in "${MODULES[@]}"; do
  if [ -d "app/modules/${m}" ]; then
    log_ok "Module directory exists: ${m}"
  else
    log_warn "Missing module directory: ${m}"
  fi
done

# -------------------------------------------------
# 3. Check Python syntax
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 3. Checking Python syntax ==="

if command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  PYTHON=python
elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  PYTHON=python3
else
  log_error "Python not found, skipping Python syntax checks"
  PYTHON=""
fi

if [ -n "${PYTHON}" ]; then
  while IFS= read -r -d '' file; do
    if "${PYTHON}" -m py_compile "${file}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      log_ok "Python syntax OK: ${file}"
    else
      log_error "Python syntax error: ${file}"
    fi
  done < <(find app -type f -name "*.py" -print0)
else
  log_warn "Skipping Python syntax validation"
fi

# -------------------------------------------------
# 4. Check TypeScript / Next.js syntax (basic)
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 4. Checking TypeScript / Next.js project ==="

if [ -f "apps/web/package.json" ]; then
  log_ok "Frontend package.json exists"
else
  log_warn "Missing apps/web/package.json"
fi

if command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v npm >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  if [ -f "apps/web/package.json" ]; then
    cd apps/web
    if npm run typecheck >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      log_ok "Frontend typecheck passed"
    else
      log_warn "Frontend typecheck failed or not configured"
    fi
    cd "${PROJECT_DIR}"
  fi
else
  log_warn "Node/npm not found, skipping TypeScript checks"
fi

# -------------------------------------------------
# 5. Check YAML syntax (docker-compose, prometheus, alertmanager)
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 5. Checking YAML syntax ==="

check_yaml() {
  local file="$1"
  if [ ! -f "${file}" ]; then
    log_warn "YAML file missing: ${file}"
    return
  fi
  if command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PY=python
  elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PY=python3
  else
    log_warn "Python not found, skipping YAML check for ${file}"
    return
  fi

  if "${PY}" -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('${file}'))" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    log_ok "YAML syntax OK: ${file}"
  else
    log_error "YAML syntax error: ${file}"
  fi
}

check_yaml "docker-compose.yml"
check_yaml "docker-compose.monitoring.yml"
check_yaml "infra/prometheus/prometheus.yml"
check_yaml "infra/alertmanager/alertmanager.yml"
check_yaml "infra/loki/loki-config.yml"
check_yaml "infra/loki/promtail-config.yml"

# -------------------------------------------------
# 6. Check JSON syntax (Grafana dashboards)
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 6. Checking JSON syntax (Grafana dashboards) ==="

check_json() {
  local file="$1"
  if [ ! -f "${file}" ]; then
    log_warn "JSON file missing: ${file}"
    return
  fi
  if command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PY=python
  elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    PY=python3
  else
    log_warn "Python not found, skipping JSON check for ${file}"
    return
  fi

  if "${PY}" -c "import json; json.load(open('${file}'))" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    log_ok "JSON syntax OK: ${file}"
  else
    log_error "JSON syntax error: ${file}"
  fi
}

for f in infra/grafana/dashboards/*.json; do
  [ -f "${f}" ] && check_json "${f}"
done

# -------------------------------------------------
# 7. Check Nginx config syntax
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 7. Checking Nginx configuration syntax ==="

if command -v nginx >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  if [ -f "infra/nginx/nginx.conf" ]; then
    if nginx -t -c "${PROJECT_DIR}/infra/nginx/nginx.conf" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      log_ok "Nginx config syntax OK"
    else
      log_error "Nginx config syntax error"
    fi
  else
    log_warn "infra/nginx/nginx.conf missing"
  fi
else
  log_warn "nginx binary not found, skipping Nginx syntax check"
fi

# -------------------------------------------------
# 8. Check Docker Compose consistency
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 8. Checking Docker Compose consistency ==="

if command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v docker compose >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  if [ -f "docker-compose.yml" ]; then
    if docker compose config >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      log_ok "docker-compose.yml is valid"
    else
      log_error "docker-compose.yml validation failed"
    fi
  fi

  if [ -f "docker-compose.monitoring.yml" ]; then
    if docker compose -f docker-compose.monitoring.yml config >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      log_ok "docker-compose.monitoring.yml is valid"
    else
      log_error "docker-compose.monitoring.yml validation failed"
    fi
  fi
else
  log_warn "Docker Compose not available, skipping compose validation"
fi

# -------------------------------------------------
# 9. Check .env.production keys
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 9. Checking .env.production consistency ==="

ENV_FILE=".env.production"
if [ -f "${ENV_FILE}" ]; then
  REQUIRED_KEYS=(
    "APP_ENV"
    "APP_DOMAIN"
    "API_DOMAIN"
    "POSTGRES_DB"
    "POSTGRES_USER"
    "POSTGRES_PASSWORD"
    "DATABASE_URL"
    "REDIS_HOST"
    "MINIO_ROOT_USER"
    "MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD"
    "S3_ENDPOINT_URL"
    "JWT_SECRET_KEY"
    "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET_KEY"
    "CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS"
    "NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL"
  )

  for key in "${REQUIRED_KEYS[@]}"; do
    if grep -q "^${key}=" "${ENV_FILE}"; then
      log_ok "Env key present: ${key}"
    else
      log_warn "Missing env key: ${key}"
    fi
  done
else
  log_error ".env.production missing"
fi

# -------------------------------------------------
# 10. Check cross-module references (basic)
# -------------------------------------------------
echo ""
echo "=== 10. Checking basic cross-module references ==="

if grep -q "from app.shared.presentation.health_router import router as health_router" app/main.py; then
  log_ok "main.py imports health_router"
else
  log_warn "main.py may not import health_router correctly"

if grep -q "from app.shared.presentation.metrics_router import router as metrics_router" app/main.py; then

  log_warn "main.py may not import metrics_router correctly"

if grep -q "from app.shared.presentation.api_router import api_router" app/main.py; then
  log_ok "main.py imports api_router"

fi

if [ -f "app/shared/presentation/health_router.py" ]; then
  if grep -q 'router = APIRouter' app/shared/presentation/health_router.py; then
    log_ok "health_router defines APIRouter"
  else
    log_warn "health_router may not define APIRouter correctly"
  fi
else
  log_error "app/shared/presentation/health_router.py missing"
fi

if [ -f "app/shared/presentation/metrics_router.py" ]; then
  if grep -q 'router = APIRouter' app/shared/presentation/metrics_router.py; then
    log_ok "metrics_router defines APIRouter"
  else
    log_warn "metrics_router may not define APIRouter correctly"
  fi
else
  log_error "app/shared/presentation/metrics_router.py missing"
fi

  "scripts/restore.sh"
  "scripts/health-check.sh"
  "scripts/logs.sh"

  if [ -f "${s}" ]; then
    log_ok "Script exists: ${s}"
    if [ -x "${s}" ]; then
      log_ok "Script is executable: ${s}"
    else
      log_warn "Script not executable: ${s}"
    fi
  else
    log_warn "Missing script: ${s}"
  fi

if [ "${ERRORS}" -gt 0 ]; then

else

fi

--- ./setup_postgres.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
set -e

echo "=== 1. Nastavenie PGDATA ==="
export PGDATA=$PREFIX/var/lib/postgresql
mkdir -p $PGDATA

echo "=== 2. Inicializácia databázy (ak neexistuje) ==="
if [ ! -f "$PGDATA/PG_VERSION" ]; then
    initdb $PGDATA
else
    echo "Databáza už bola inicializovaná."
fi

echo "=== 3. Spustenie PostgreSQL servera ==="
if ! pg_ctl -D $PGDATA status > /dev/null 2>&1; then
    pg_ctl -D $PGDATA -l logfile start
else
    echo "PostgreSQL už beží."
fi

echo "=== 4. Vytvorenie používateľa a databázy ==="
createuser -s recyclehub 2>/dev/null || true
createdb -O recyclehub recyclehub 2>/dev/null || true
psql -d recyclehub -c "ALTER USER recyclehub WITH PASSWORD 'recyclehub';" 2>/dev/null || true

echo "=== 5. Spustenie Alembic migrácií ==="
alembic upgrade head

echo "=== Hotovo! Databáza je pripravená. ==="

--- ./start-platform.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
ROOT="$HOME/waste-platform"
cd "$ROOT"

echo "=== CMEP start ==="

# Postgres
if ! pg_isready -h localhost -p 5432 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "[pg] starting..."
  pg_ctl -D "$PREFIX/var/lib/postgresql" start || true
  sleep 2
fi
pg_isready -h localhost -p 5432 && echo "[pg] ok" || echo "[pg] FAIL"

# Redis
if ! redis-cli ping 2>/dev/null | grep -q PONG; then
  echo "[redis] starting..."
  redis-server --daemonize yes || true
  sleep 1
fi
redis-cli ping 2>/dev/null | grep -q PONG && echo "[redis] ok" || echo "[redis] FAIL"

# stop old
pkill -f "uvicorn app.main:app" 2>/dev/null || true
pkill -f "next dev" 2>/dev/null || true
sleep 1

# backend
echo "[backend] :8001"
nohup uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8001 --reload \
  > "$HOME/cmep-backend.log" 2>&1 &
BACKEND_PID=$!
echo "backend pid $BACKEND_PID"

# frontend
echo "[frontend] :3001"
cd "$ROOT/apps/web"
nohup npm run dev -- --port 3001 --hostname 0.0.0.0 \
  > "$HOME/cmep-frontend.log" 2>&1 &
FRONTEND_PID=$!
echo "frontend pid $FRONTEND_PID"

# Počkaj 5 sekúnd na naštartovanie
sleep 5

# Skontroluj backend (stačí raz)
if curl -s -m 5 http://localhost:8001/health >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "✅ backend ready"
else
  echo "❌ backend not responding (check ~/cmep-backend.log)"
fi

# Skontroluj frontend – opakovane až 30 pokusov
echo "Čakám na frontend..."
for i in {1..30}; do
  if curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" http://localhost:3001 | grep -q "^2"; then
    echo "✅ frontend ready (HTTP 200)"
    break
  fi
  if [ $i -eq 30 ]; then
    echo "❌ frontend not ready after 30s (check ~/cmep-frontend.log)"
  fi
  sleep 1
done

echo ""
echo "API:  http://localhost:8001/docs"
echo "WEB:  http://localhost:3001"
echo "Logy: tail -f ~/cmep-backend.log ~/cmep-frontend.log"

--- ./start-postgres-safe.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -u

PREFIX="${PREFIX:-/data/data/com.termux/files/usr}"
PGDATA="${PREFIX}/var/lib/postgresql"
PGLOG="${HOME}/postgresql-waste-platform.log"

echo '===== TIME ====='
date -u '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'
echo

echo '===== POSTGRES BINARIES ====='
command -v postgres || true
command -v pg_ctl || true
command -v initdb || true
command -v pg_isready || true
echo

echo '===== PGDATA ====='
echo "PGDATA=$PGDATA"
if [ -d "$PGDATA" ]; then
    ls -ld "$PGDATA"
    find "$PGDATA" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -printf '%f
' 2>/dev/null | sort || true
else
    echo "PGDATA_MISSING"
fi
echo

echo '===== EXISTING SERVER STATUS ====='
pg_ctl -D "$PGDATA" status 2>&1 || true
echo

if [ ! -f "$PGDATA/PG_VERSION" ]; then
    echo '===== NO INITIALIZED CLUSTER ====='
    echo 'PG_VERSION_MISSING'
    echo 'ACTION_REQUIRED=initdb'
    exit 2
fi

echo '===== STARTING EXISTING CLUSTER ====='
pg_ctl -D "$PGDATA" -l "$PGLOG" -o "-h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432" start -w 2>&1 || true
echo

echo '===== SERVER STATUS AFTER START ====='
pg_ctl -D "$PGDATA" status 2>&1 || true
echo

echo '===== READINESS ====='
pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1 -p 5432 || true
echo

echo '===== POSTGRES LOG ====='
tail -n 100 "$PGLOG" 2>/dev/null || true

--- ./step1_remove_backups.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
echo "KROK 1: ODSTRAŇUJEM ZÁLOHY A DOČASNÉ SÚBORY"
cd ~/waste-platform || exit 1

# Odstránenie všetkých .bak, .backup, .bak-*, .orig, .old
find . -type f \( -name "*.bak" -o -name "*.backup" -o -name "*.bak-*" -o -name "*.orig" -o -name "*.old" \) -delete

# Odstránenie __pycache__ a .pyc
find . -type d -name "__pycache__" -exec rm -rf {} \; 2>/dev/null
find . -name "*.pyc" -delete

# Odstránenie prázdnych adresárov
find . -type d -empty -delete

echo "HOTOVO. Zálohy a dočasné súbory boli odstránené."

--- ./step5_fix_frontend_auth.sh ---
#!/bin/bash
echo "KROK 5: OPRAVA FRONTEND TOKEN STORE"
cd ~/waste-platform/apps/web || exit 1

cp src/lib/auth/token-store.ts src/lib/auth/token-store.ts.bak
cp src/lib/api/http.ts src/lib/api/http.ts.bak

cat > src/lib/auth/token-store.ts << 'INNER_EOF'
export const tokenStore = {
  getToken: (): string | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    return localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  },
  setToken: (token: string): void => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.setItem('accessToken', token);
  },
  clearToken: (): void => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.removeItem('accessToken');
    localStorage.removeItem('refreshToken');
  },
  getRefreshToken: (): string | null => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    return localStorage.getItem('refreshToken');
  },
  setRefreshToken: (token: string): void => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    localStorage.setItem('refreshToken', token);
  },
};
INNER_EOF

cat > src/lib/api/http.ts << 'INNER_EOF'
import { tokenStore } from '../auth/token-store';

async function request<T>(path: string, options: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const baseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:8001';
  const url = `${baseUrl}${path.startsWith('/') ? path : '/' + path}`;
  const token = tokenStore.getToken();

  const headers: HeadersInit = {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    ...(options.headers || {}),
  };

  if (token) {
    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  const response = await fetch(url, { ...options, headers });

  if (!response.ok) {
    if (response.status === 401) {
      tokenStore.clearToken();
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.location.href = '/login';
    }
    const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw {
      message: errorData.detail || errorData.message || 'An unexpected error occurred',
      status: response.status,
      errors: errorData.errors,
    };
  }
  if (response.status === 204) return {} as T;
  return response.json();
}

export const http = {
  get: <T>(path: string, options?: RequestInit) => request<T>(path, { ...options, method: 'GET' }),
  post: <T>(path: string, body?: any, options?: RequestInit) => request<T>(path, { ...options, method: 'POST', body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined }),
  put: <T>(path: string, body?: any, options?: RequestInit) => request<T>(path, { ...options, method: 'PUT', body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined }),
  patch: <T>(path: string, body?: any, options?: RequestInit) => request<T>(path, { ...options, method: 'PATCH', body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined }),
  delete: <T>(path: string, options?: RequestInit) => request<T>(path, { ...options, method: 'DELETE' }),
};
INNER_EOF

echo "HOTOVO. Reštartuj frontend: cd apps/web && npm run dev"

--- ./test-next-prod-vs-dev.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -eu

echo "===== PRODUCTION BUILD ====="
npm run build > "$HOME/cmep-next-build.log" 2>&1 || true
tail -n 200 "$HOME/cmep-next-build.log" || true

echo
echo "===== START PRODUCTION SERVER ====="
pkill -f "next start" 2>/dev/null || true
nohup npm run start > "$HOME/cmep-next-start.log" 2>&1 &
sleep 8

echo
echo "===== PROD SERVER LOG ====="
tail -n 200 "$HOME/cmep-next-start.log" || true

echo "===== CURL PROD LOCAL ROOT ====="
curl -I --max-time 15 http://127.0.0.1:3001/ || true

echo
echo "===== CURL PROD LOCAL LOGIN ====="
curl -I --max-time 15 http://127.0.0.1:3001/login || true

echo
echo "===== CURL PROD LOCAL BODY LOGIN ====="
curl --max-time 15 http://127.0.0.1:3001/login | head -n 40 || true

--- ./verify-db-and-login.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
set -u

export PGPASSWORD='recyclehub'

echo '===== DATABASE CONNECTION ====='
psql \
  -h 127.0.0.1 \
  -p 5432 \
  -U recyclehub \
  -d recyclehub \
  -c 'select current_database(), current_user, version();' || true
echo

echo '===== DATABASE TABLES ====='
psql \
  -h 127.0.0.1 \
  -p 5432 \
  -U recyclehub \
  -d recyclehub \
  -c 'dt' || true
echo

echo '===== API HEALTH ====='
curl -i -m 10 http://localhost:8010/health || true
echo

echo '===== API LOGIN ====='
curl -i -m 10 -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/auth/login \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"nonexistent@example.com","password":"wrongpass"}' || true
echo

echo '===== CLEAN BACKEND LOG ====='
