--- ./waste_platform_python_audit.sh ---
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

set +e

PROJECT="$HOME/waste-platform"

echo "============================================================"
echo " WASTE PLATFORM - PYTHON DEPENDENCY AUDIT"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo "Projekt: $PROJECT"
echo "Dátum:   $(date)"
echo

cd "$PROJECT" || exit 1

echo
echo "=== 1. PYTHON ==="
python --version
python -m pip --version

echo
echo "=== 2. PROJEKTOVÉ SÚBORY ==="
find . -maxdepth 3 -type f \
    \( -name 'requirements*.txt' \
    -o -name 'pyproject.toml' \
    -o -name 'setup.py' \
    -o -name 'setup.cfg' \
    -o -name 'Pipfile' \
    -o -name 'poetry.lock' \
    -o -name 'uv.lock' \
    \) \
    -print 2>/dev/null

echo
echo "=== 3. GIT ==="
git status --short 2>/dev/null
echo
git branch --show-current 2>/dev/null
echo
git log -1 --oneline 2>/dev/null

echo
echo "=== 4. PYTHON SÚBORY ==="
find . -type f -name '*.py' \
    -not -path './.git/*' \
    -not -path './venv/*' \
    -not -path './.venv/*' \
    | sort | head -200

echo
echo "=== 5. IMPORTY V PROJEKTE ==="

python - <<'PY'
import ast
import pathlib
import collections

root = pathlib.Path(".")
imports = collections.Counter()

for path in root.rglob("*.py"):
    if any(part in {".git", "venv", ".venv", "__pycache__"} for part in path.parts):
        continue

    try:
        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(errors="ignore"))
    except Exception:
        continue

    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            for n in node.names:
                imports[n.name.split(".")[0]] += 1

        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            if node.module:
                imports[node.module.split(".")[0]] += 1

for name, count in imports.most_common():
    print(f"{name:35} {count}")
PY

echo
echo "=== 6. PIP CHECK ==="
python -m pip check

echo
echo "=== 7. PYTHON BALÍKY ==="
python -m pip list --format=columns

echo
echo "=== 8. ENVIRONMENT ==="
env | grep -E '^(PYTHON|PATH|VIRTUAL_ENV|PIP)' | sort

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " AUDIT HOTOVÝ - NIČ SA NEINŠTALOVALO ANI NEODSTRAŇOVALO"
echo "============================================================"

===== 21 DOCKER / DEPLOYMENT =====
--- docker-compose.yml ---
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16-alpine
    container_name: cmep-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER:-cmep}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?POSTGRES_PASSWORD musi byt nastaveny}
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB:-cmep}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${POSTGRES_USER:-cmep} -d ${POSTGRES_DB:-cmep}"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5
    restart: unless-stopped
    networks:
      - cmep-network

  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    container_name: cmep-redis
    volumes:
      - redis_data:/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 5
    restart: unless-stopped
    networks:
      - cmep-network

  api:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: app/Dockerfile
    container_name: cmep-api
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql+asyncpg://${POSTGRES_USER:-cmep}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD:?POSTGRES_PASSWORD musi byt nastaveny}@postgres:5432/${POSTGRES_DB:-cmep}
      REDIS_HOST: redis
      JWT_SECRET_KEY: ${JWT_SECRET_KEY:?JWT_SECRET_KEY musi byt nastaveny}
      JWT_REFRESH_SECRET_KEY: ${JWT_REFRESH_SECRET_KEY:?JWT_REFRESH_SECRET_KEY musi byt nastaveny}
      CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: ${CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:-http://localhost:3001}
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      redis:
        condition: service_healthy
    restart: unless-stopped
    networks:
      - cmep-network

  web:
    build:
      context: ./apps/web
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: cmep-web
    environment:
      NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL: ${NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL:-http://localhost:8000}
    depends_on:
      api:
        condition: service_started
    restart: unless-stopped
    networks:
      - cmep-network

  cmep-network:
    driver: bridge

  postgres_data:
  redis_data:

--- docker/backend/Dockerfile ---
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app

# Inštalácia systémových závislostí
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    gcc \
    libpq-dev \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Kopírovanie requirements
COPY backend/requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

# Kopírovanie kódu
COPY backend/ .

# Vytvorenie užívateľa
RUN useradd -m -u 1000 appuser && chown -R appuser:appuser /app
USER appuser

# Spustenie
CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000", "--reload"]

--- docker/frontend/Dockerfile ---
FROM node:18-alpine

WORKDIR /app

# Kopírovanie package.json
COPY frontend/package*.json ./
RUN npm ci

# Kopírovanie kódu
COPY frontend/ .

# Build
RUN npm run build

# Spustenie
CMD ["npm", "start"]

--- docker/nginx/Dockerfile ---
FROM nginx:alpine

# Kopírovanie konfigurácie
COPY docker/nginx/conf.d/default.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
COPY docker/nginx/ssl /etc/nginx/ssl

# Health check
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=3s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD wget --quiet --tries=1 --spider http://localhost/health || exit 1

EXPOSE 80 443

--- docker/nginx/conf.d/cmep.conf ---
upstream cmep_api {
    server api:8000;
}
upstream cmep_web {
    server web:3001;
}

server {
    listen 80;
    server_name _;

    client_max_body_size 20m;

    location /api/ {
        proxy_pass http://cmep_api;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }

    location /health {
        proxy_pass http://cmep_api/health;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
    }

    location /docs {
        proxy_pass http://cmep_api/docs;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
    }

    location /openapi.json {
        proxy_pass http://cmep_api/openapi.json;
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
    }

    location / {
        proxy_pass http://cmep_web;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
    }
}

          url: "$${__value.raw}"

groups:
  - name: cmep-platform-alerts
    rules:
      - alert: ApiDown
        expr: up{job="api"} == 0
        for: 2m
        labels:
          severity: critical
        annotations:
          summary: "API is down"
          description: "FastAPI metrics endpoint has been unreachable for more than 2 minutes."
